The initial cut applied the mirror as a click→stage flip in `pixel_to_stage_offset` + mosaic `_orient_tile` with the display left un-mirrored; reworked to flip at the source (above) so the DISPLAY is corrected and the mapping/mosaic drop their mirror terms — `pixel_to_stage_offset` and the mosaic builder now apply rotation only.]** **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1/V3** (calibrate each camera's rotation → plausible angles ≈ nominal; toggle Mirrored view on a flipped feed → live-view clicks drive the stage the right way; 4th Monitor slot assigns/starts/previews; readouts + mirror restore after restart). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_camera_rotation_cal_and_monitor.py` (35: 22 rotation/monitor + 13 mirror) + camera-store/liveview/rotation/objective/role/reanchor/correction/hw-controls/async-open/fluorescence/hardware-page suites green (one hard-coded 3-slot expectation in `test_v744_calibration_revision` made `MAX_LIVE_CAMERAS`-agnostic). Pre-existing unrelated: `test_v75x_spheroid_picker_scaling` 5 errors (`CameraManager.__new__` fake lacking `_rotation_deg`, from concurrent working-tree WIP — confirmed with this change stashed). |
| `MEBP_v75x_STARTUP_CAMERA_FREEZE_AND_CONTEXT_PANEL_WIDTH.md` | v7.5.x two startup-usability fixes (operator: "the camera boot-up freezes the UI" + "the left context menu can't be moved out fully so I can't see many of the buttons; it goes away if I stretch the window wider"). **(1) Camera freeze:** the OpenCV *probe* was already backgrounded (`detect_cameras_async`) but the *device open* still ran on the GUI thread — `_start_saved_cameras` (startup auto-start of saved cameras) → `CameraManager.start` → `CameraWidget.start()` → the multi-second `cv2.VideoCapture(idx, CAP_DSHOW)` / `ToupCamBackend.open` / `AndorBackend.open` — freezing the event loop (and every `QTimer` camera feed) until each opened. **Fix:** new `CameraWidget.start_async(on_done=)` opens on a daemon thread and marshals the opened handle back to the GUI thread via a new private `_open_result` signal → `_on_open_result` assigns it + starts the display `QTimer` there; `_release_handle`; `_open_token`/`_opening`/`_pending_on_done` state; `stop()` bumps `_open_token` so an in-flight open is **superseded** (result discarded + handle released → a stop/restart mid-open can't leave a camera streaming). Simulated/already-running/unavailable finish synchronously. New `CameraManager.start_async` routes to it, emitting `camera_started` from the completion callback (same signal contract); **`CameraManager.start` kept SYNCHRONOUS** because the mosaic-scan path grabs a frame immediately after start. `_start_saved_cameras` now uses `start_async`. Enumeration (DirectShow/ToupCam/Andor) left on the GUI thread (COM prefers the main thread; it's the fast part). **(2) Context panel width:** the left box had `minimumWidth = s(340)` and a dynamic max `total − reserve` with `_content_reserve_px = s(460)`; on any window narrower than ~`min+reserve` (×1.5 at 150 % DPI) the max dropped below the min, so the panel opened at 340 (< its designed 540) and clipped the buttons, and couldn't be dragged. **First fix (`app.py`):** content pane (slot 1) made **non-collapsible** (yields by shrinking/scrolling, no "snap to full open"); `_content_reserve_px` reduced 460→240. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1/V3** (UI responsive while saved cameras come up + all feeds stream). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_camera_async_open_and_context_width.py` (8: async open success/fail/supersede-on-stop, manager `start_async` emits when running, sync `start` intact, bounds never-below-min/grows) + updated `test_v75x_camera_calibration_store.py` (stub `start_async`); camera/context regression 141 green. **Addendum (2026-07-24) — responsive "sized to fit" content (operator: "content of the left context menu … sized to fit … adaptive text/button sizing, almost like a css file for html" → then "now it looks terrible … jog axis sections should be aligned … sized to fit the width … no scrolling … its ok to scrunch the buttons, we just need to make sure the text gets smaller as well"):** a first FlowLayout-wrap attempt looked ragged → **removed** (`flow_layout.py` deleted). Final = **aligned grid, fit-to-width, no horizontal scroll**. New **`gui/widgets/responsive.py`** (`container_scale(width,design,lo,hi)` = CSS `clamp()` on top of DPI `scale_factor()`; `quantize`). `jog_button_array.py` rewritten: the 3 step rows (XY/Z/P) share **ONE `QGridLayout`** (col 0 label · cols 1-5 magnitude buttons · col 6 custom) so labels + button columns align across rows; magnitude buttons/custom/pump columns are `Expanding` w/ equal column stretch → always fill+fit the width (scrunch when narrow); direction pad = centred cluster. font-size/min-width pulled out of the buttons' QSS so `resizeEvent`→`container_scale(width,_DESIGN_BASE=470,0.62,1.15)`→`_apply_scale` rescales every label/button/field point-size + height + label-col width + pad-button size (**text shrinks with the width**, centralised `_BASE_*`/`_FONT_*`); **public API + jog signal contract unchanged** (verified + rendered offscreen at 260/380/540 px). `app.py` context-box min `s(540)`→**`s(260)`** (still opens at `LEFT_BOX_WIDTH`); `context_panel_host` native `QScrollArea` gets `ScrollBarAlwaysOff` (horizontal). Vertical scroll stays (full panel taller than most windows — eliminating it needs collapsible sections/tabs, flagged for operator). Nav sidebar left as-is (labels fit its 200 px expanded width). **Rev 2 (operator: "min width should be 100px and each item should take up a %% of the width"):** made the WHOLE panel proportional + fit to 100 px — box min `s(260)`→**`s(100)`**; jog buttons/fields/pumps keep equal column stretch with tiny minimums (stretch drives width, font floor lowered to 4.5 pt so text keeps shrinking); speed rows / position rows / Absolute-Go-To / Hardware-Info made proportional (`Ignored` h-policy + tiny min + stretch), section headings `setWordWrap`, Refresh + safety-warning shrinkable; `Card` title uses `Ignored`+tiny-min so a long title elides not floors; and `JogButtonArray` / `HardwareControlPanel` / `StandardJogContextPanel` each override `minimumSizeHint()` to a small width so the scroll area can drive them to 100 px (natural min 338→~96). Verified offscreen through a real `QScrollArea` host at 100/130/200/320 px — fully proportional, no horizontal scroll. **Rev 3 (operator: "why can't the items just get smaller by a %% of the width?"; AskUserQuestion → scale text, not a QGraphicsView zoom):** Rev 2 scaled the jog buttons' fonts but let the other sections *clip* their text; Rev 3 makes it uniform — new `responsive.scale_descendant_fonts(root, factor, skip_subtrees=)` (records each widget's base pt once, sets `pointSizeF = base×factor`), called from `HardwareControlPanel.resizeEvent` + `StandardJogContextPanel.resizeEvent` with `factor=container_scale(width, s(440), 0.45, 1.12)` (control panel skips the self-scaling `JogButtonArray`; context panel skips the self-scaling `HardwareControlPanel`). Stripped hard-coded `font-size` from the resolved-speed label + Hardware-Info rows' QSS so they scale (QSS font-size beats setFont); the verbose safety-warning banner now scales down too instead of dominating. Verified offscreen at 140/240/340 px (`_font_factor` 0.44→0.54→0.78, all text scales together, no clipping). Tests: `test_v75x_responsive_context_panel.py` (15, +`TestDescendantFontScaling`) green at 1.0× and 1.5×. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_JOG_OFF_GUI_THREAD.md` | v7.5.x fix — "when I use the 3D printer board to move the pump, the cameras stop displaying frames." Root cause: `HardwareControlPanel._on_jog_pump` (the shared pump-jog handler behind the "Jog" pill panel used everywhere, and Hardware Setup) called `StageController.move_pump_uL(...)` synchronously on the GUI thread; with `device_profile.backlash_comp_enabled: true` (set on ME3B V3), that brackets the click with a TAKE-UP → MAIN → UNLOAD sequence, each sub-move **blocking** via an M400 drain + settle dwell — up to several seconds on the Qt event loop, during which no `QTimer`-driven camera feed (`camera_widget.py`) can tick, so the live view appears frozen. Same class of bug as `MEBP_v75x_JOG_TRAVEL_OFF_GUI_THREAD.md`. **Fix:** `_on_jog_pump` now runs the actual `move_pump_uL`/`move_pump_relative` call on a daemon thread (mirrors the existing `SafeTravelWorker` / `calibration.py::_compcal_step_move` idiom) with a busy-guard (overlapping clicks ignored, not queued) and a queued `_pump_jog_done` Signal back to the GUI thread for the post-move position refresh. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V3** (jog a pump with backlash comp on while watching a live camera feed — no freeze). Tests: updated `test_v75x_axis_max_speed_inputs.py` / `test_v75x_common_axis_speed_source.py` (wait for the backgrounded move before asserting); 62 tests green; pump-compliance/plunger-setup/jog-direction suites green (1 pre-existing unrelated failure, confirmed present with this change stashed). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRIOR_III_BAUD_AND_MULTI_BAUD_DETECT.md` | v7.5.x fix — "the Prior ProScan III is connected for sure, but the software says it can't find it" (ME3B V3, after swapping the Ludl MAC 5000 back out for a Prior III). **Two independent root causes, both found via live read-only diagnostics:** (1) baud mismatch — `proscan_iii.json` assumes 38400, this unit (`H117P1N4/F` via `STAGE`) was sitting at 9600 (Prior's manual: no DIP switch for baud exists — confirmed absent from the full manual; it's a firmware/EEPROM setting that **reverts to 9600 if the port sits idle across 2 power cycles**, the likely cause); (2) this unit's `V` (firmware_version) query returns a bare `R` instead of a version string (confirmed at both 9600 and 38400 — a genuine firmware behavior, not a baud artifact), so `proscan_iii.json`'s V-based detection could never match it regardless of baud. **Hardware fix (live, 2026-07-23):** researched + confirmed Prior's own documented `BAUD,<code>` command (`96`/`19`/`38`/`115` → 9600/19200/38400/115200, acks `0`) against Prior's official ProScan II/III manuals; stepped the unit 9600→38400→115200 (max, Rev-F/USB-FTDI per the `/F` suffix), verifying communication after each step. **Software:** new `config/controllers/proscan_iii_h117.json` (same Prior III wire syntax, verified identical `format_command` output to `proscan_iii.json`) with `STAGE`-based detection (`identify_tokens: ["STAGE =","MICROSTEPS"]`, `wake_delay_ms: 300` — 300ms alone truncated the multi-line reply, ~600ms settle needed) and `communication.baud_rates: [115200,38400,9600]`; new **`ControllerProtocol.baud_rate_candidates`** (absent ⇒ `[baud_rate]`, byte-identical for every existing protocol) + `XYStage._find_with_protocol` now retries every candidate baud per port — resilience against the exact fail-safe reversion that caused this, not just a one-time fix. Named after the stage model (`_h117`), not the baud, since the baud changed twice mid-session. **Also found+fixed in the same investigation:** the Device-page XY-controller combo (`_on_xy_controller_changed`) persisted to `settings.json` but never called `_persist_active_profile()` (unlike "Save XY Calibration"), so the on-disk device profile stayed stale and a later profile Load would silently revert a fresh controller choice — fixed there and in the equivalent gap in `settings_page.py`. **A mistake made and recovered during this session:** an errant `Settings()` call without `.load()` wiped `settings.json` to near-defaults (`device_profile.active`, calibration section, etc.) — recovered cleanly via `git restore --source=HEAD -- settings.json` (the file was tracked before `.gitignore` added it) per the operator's own suggestion; verified byte-identical to HEAD apart from the one intended field. All settings/profile writes now use the load-modify-save pattern, never a bare constructor. Tests: `test_v75x_prior_multi_baud_detect.py` (8) + full Ludl/jog/speed/axis-map regression (114) green; real-HW verified end-to-end through `XYStageManager` (detected, connected at 115200, live position + speed/accel readback all correct). |
| `MEBP_v75x_LUDL_MAC5000_XY_CONTROLLER.md` | v7.5.x **new XY-stage controller: Ludl LEP MAC 5000** alongside Prior ProScan II/III (operator: "we have a new type of xy stage … using a LEP MAC 5000 controller"; ensure correct commands + consistent speed/accel/travel per stage). The XY layer was a JSON-protocol abstraction but **5 behaviors were hardcoded Prior-isms** in `XYStage.py`; this generalizes them **protocol-driven, defaulting to Prior so `proscan_ii/iii.json` are byte-for-byte unchanged** (a regression test freezes that). **Decisions (AskUserQuestion):** per-machine + auto-detect · research + tunable · build a sim. **`ControllerProtocol`** gains optional getters (all Prior-safe defaults): `family` (`prior`/`ludl`), `command_mode_init_bytes` (from `command_mode_init_hex`, e.g. Ludl `FF41` = raw `0xFF 0x41` sent at connect to force ASCII/high-level mode), `ack_success_token`+`match_ack_error` (Prior bare `R` vs Ludl `:A`/`:N -<code>`), `get_position_parse` (Prior `x,y,z` CSV+strip-`R` vs Ludl `WHERE X Y`→`:A <x> <y>` whitespace/2-axis), `speed_model`/`speed_units`/`speed_is_per_axis`, `accel_model`, `position_scale` (stage wire-units per µm, Prior 1.0 / Ludl ≈10 counts/µm). **`XYStage`** applies the scale on move send + inverse on parse (`_position_scale`), `_drain_ack` (Prior `R` drain / Ludl `:A`/`:N` recognize, non-fatal), `_parse_position_response` now a protocol-dispatched **instance** method, `set_speed_mm_s`/`set_velocity` branch on `speed_model` (`_set_speed_absolute` → per-axis `SPEED` counts/s, clamped to the µm/s ceiling AND `speed_range` for safety), serial open reads `stop_bits` (Ludl 2) + writes the init bytes, `__init__` sim branch picks `LudlStageSimulator` when `family=="ludl"`. **Per-machine selection:** `DeviceProfile.xy_controller_json` (+`xy_max_speed_um_s`) round-trips and writes into the SAME global `controller.controller_json` key on apply (precedence device profile → global → auto-detect; None = inherit); `StageController.set_controller_json` applies on the next XY connect (no restart); Hardware Setup → Device "XY controller" combo + accel range/label relabel per model (Prior 1-100 / Ludl 1-255 ramp); settings-page dropdown pushes it too. **New `config/controllers/mac5000.json`** (researched from Micro-Manager `Ludl.cpp` + shared ASI/LEP command set: `MOVE`/`MOVREL`/`WHERE`/`HERE`/`SPEED`/`ACCEL`/`HALT`/`VER`, `:A`/`:N` acks, CR term, 9600-8-N-2, counts) with bench-TBD defaults flagged (µm/count, SPEED units pulses/s-vs-mm/s, stop bits). **New `SupportClasses/LudlStageSimulator.py`** (standalone — zero regression to the Prior sim; µm internally, counts on the wire, `:A` acks, own `sim_xy_state_ludl.json`). Ludl HLC has **no continuous vector-jog** (`set_velocity` null) — rather than leave Xbox-stick jog a dead no-op, `move_stage_at_velocity` detects the missing command and delegates to new `XYStage._jog_pulse` (integrates the commanded velocity over wall-clock time into an accumulator, fires a real `move_stage_relative`/`MOVREL` once the delta clears one wire count; resets on stick-release) — click/point-to-point jog was already unaffected. Travel envelope stays µm-native (`SafetyLimits` clamps in µm ABOVE `XYStage`; scale only inside → `plate_axis_sign`/`plate_flip_180` unaffected). **Real-HW verified 2026-07-22 on COM5** (an FTDI USB-serial, confirmed 9600-8-N-2 + `0xFF 0x41` init; `VER`→`Version no. : 5.100 MAC5000 Interface with USB`; `WHERE X Y`→`:A 399 321`; detection fixed to probe with `WHERE X Y` not `VER`, since the real VER reply isn't `:A`-prefixed; a live `MOVREL` executed the exact commanded count delta) — **and the connect-time port scan was fixed to never OPEN the ZP/Marlin's COM port** (`XYStageManager(exclude_ports=…)`, fed from `StageController.zp_connected_port`/`_preferred_zp_port`) since merely opening a serial port asserts DTR and auto-resets an Arduino/Marlin board. Still needs a live-Xbox bench check of the pulsed jog's feel, plus `xy_position_scale`/`speed_units` bench-tuning against a measured distance. **VE/VECTOR (Prior-style continuous-velocity) tested LIVE and confirmed unsupported** (`:N -1` — real hardware, zero-motion probe), settling the "can it move at velocity" question definitively: no, this firmware has no such primitive; the pulsed fallback is the correct approach. **ACCEL bench-CONFIRMED backwards from typical intuition** — it's a ramp-TIME index, LOWER is FASTER (a 200 µm move: 0.61s at ACCEL=1 vs 0.88s at ACCEL=255, same SPEED) — `parameters.acceleration` default flipped 100→1, UI label/hint updated, a freshly-selected Ludl profile now defaults the spinbox to 1 instead of inheriting Prior's mid-range 50. **New live-readback capability** (motivated by a real incident: a malformed `SPEED X=0.3` corrupted only X's SPEED register to 84 counts/sec while Y stayed at 230400, with zero app-visible sign of the divergence) — `get_max_speed`/`get_acceleration` protocol commands (Prior bare `SMS`/`SAS` query; Ludl per-axis `SPEED {axis}?`/`ACCEL {axis}?`), `XYStage._query_protocol_value` (write+read, unlike the fire-and-forget `_send_protocol_command`) + `get_speed_readback`/`get_acceleration_readback` (tolerate the real Ludl reply quirk of trailing junk on single-axis queries, e.g. `:A 20 N-2`), and a new "Live Stage State" card on Hardware Setup → Device (Read-from-stage button, per-axis X/Y display, yellow mismatch flag for Ludl, collapses to one shared row for Prior) — verified against the real hardware read-only. Tests: `test_v75x_ludl_protocol_semantics.py`/`_ludl_xystage.py`/`_ludl_simulator.py`/`_ludl_xy_prior_regression.py` (60, incl. `TestExcludePorts`/`TestJogPulseFallback`/`TestReadback`) + jog/axis/speed/z-retract/z-setup (146) + calibration/orientation (58) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ILLUMINATION_LED_CONTROL.md` | v7.5.x new **dimmable microscope illumination LED** (ZF-R25-0403 12 V COB) driven off the ZP board's **FAN0** output, brightness set from the app (operator). The header is named in exactly one place — `ZPStage._LED_FAN_INDEX = 0` — so a rewire is a one-line change (extracted 2026-07-31 during a FAN2 move that was reverted the same day; the LED is on FAN0). **No axis / no device-profile change** — the LED is not motion; it rides a spare fan PWM pin. **Backend:** new `ZPStageManager.set_led_brightness(level)` (clamps 0-255, sends **`M106 P0 S<v>`** via the existing `ok`-handshaked `send_data`) + guarded `StageController.set_led_brightness` passthrough (no-op/False when `is_zp_connected` False). **No firmware flash for the default path** — FAN0 (`PC6`) is already `M106`-controllable in stock SKR Mini E3 V3.0 Marlin. ⚠ If the LED is ever moved to another header, note that FAN2 (`PB15`) is commonly an **auto/controller fan** (`E0_AUTO_FAN_PIN`/`CONTROLLER_FAN_PIN`) rather than an M106 fan, and Marlin **acks an unassigned `P` index with `ok` and does nothing** — a SILENT failure (LED dead, app reports success), so verify on a terminal before debugging software. Flash `FAST_PWM_FAN` only if bench testing bands the camera (M106 vs M355 share the pin timer — command choice doesn't fix flicker). **Bed-heater output rejected** (its slow PWM would band the imaging camera; ≤ 1 A fits the fan MOSFET). **GUI:** one reusable `gui/widgets/illumination_control.py::IlluminationControl` (on/off `QCheckBox` + 0-100 % `QSlider` + live label; ~100 ms debounce so drags don't flood the shared serial bus; every write guarded on `is_zp_connected`; `on_status_update` greys on disconnect; silent `set_on`/`set_value` for state restore) — embedded as a `Card("Illumination")` in `StandardJogContextPanel` (rides the left-box **"Jog" pill** everywhere that panel appears) **and** registered as a drop-in custom-panel module `register_section("illumination", … "💡 Illumination LED")` reusing the same widget (no host/panel/store change). **🐞 2026-08-04 — ONE WIDGET CLASS WAS NOT ONE STATE** (operator: *"if i go to different pages, the LED section on the jog pannel is each different"*): sharing the widget *class* wasn't enough — **nine** pages each build their OWN `StandardJogContextPanel` (jog/calibration/spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print/stress/timing + the Custom panel's `JogSection`), so that many `IlluminationControl`s were alive each holding the toggle/brightness in its own `QCheckBox`/`QSlider`, and **`M106` has no readback** so nothing could ever reconcile them (the very thing that makes this unlike the Microscope card beside it, which polls a real `MicroscopeState` off a shared controller). A light dimmed to 45 % on Jog read "off" on Calibration, and touching that stale control **commanded** the stale value — so navigating between pages could change the illumination. Fixed by moving the state (on/pct/remembered level/controller) into a process-wide `_IlluminationState(QObject)` (`illumination_state()`); each widget is now a VIEW rendering on `state.changed`, same public API, and Qt drops the connection on destroy so a closed page can't be rendered into (avoiding the listener-outlives-the-widget hazard the microscope panel dodged by polling). **The single WRITE matters as much as the single state:** merely cross-notifying views would have queued N debounced `M106`s per change onto the shared `ok`-blocking ZP channel — exactly the flooding the debounce exists to prevent — so the timer + send live on the state (widget `_send_timer`/`_send_now` are thin delegates). Custom-panel option restore is now **seed-once** (`is_pristine()`) because that panel rebuilds its sections on every layout change and a re-seed would reset the live LED everywhere. **⭐ 2026-08-05 — the LED is turned off on shutdown** (operator: *"when i shut down the software make sure it turns the led off"*): it's a write-only output, so a lit LED stayed lit after exit with nothing left to control it. New guarded `StageController.led_off()` called from `shutdown()` — the ONE chokepoint every exit path reaches (`MainWindow.closeEvent` + all three `main.py` headless paths) — and **placement is the substance**: after `_pos_poller.stop()` (no serial-lock contention on the way out) and before `disconnect_stages()` (afterwards the port is closed and the board unreachable), synchronous on the shutdown thread since a write racing `serial.close()` is a hard Windows crash (`MEBP_v75x_ZP_CLOSE_DURING_READ_CRASH.md`), and wrapped so a board dying mid-exit can't derail the teardown. ⚠ **Do NOT rely on the close-time DTR/RTS de-assert** — it resets the board (dropping the fan output) only on some board/driver polarities, which is why this looked like it might already work; on this rig the LED stays lit. Deliberately **NOT** extended to `disconnect_zp()` (also called from the board-drop handler and around auto-reconnect, where the LED is already dark and a self-healing reconnect would come back with the operator's light silently killed) — a manual "Disconnect ZP" still leaves the LED as-is. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (LED lights/dims/off; no camera banding at real exposures; card greys when ZP disconnected; ZP `ok` handshake stays healthy under slider spam; the LED reads the same on every page as you navigate; **closing the app darkens the LED**, with a final acked `M106 P0 S0` in `logs/zp_serial.log` before the port closes and no visible close delay). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_illumination_led.py` (**31** — +8 shared-state/cross-page, +6 shutdown incl. both orderings and the real-`ZPStageManager` wire check); **mutation-verified**: per-view state fails 4 of 6 sync tests, and removing / re-ordering / un-guarding the shutdown call each fails a distinct test. Regression green: context-panel/responsive-context/nikon-ti (137), jog-nav/zp-serial/workflow-settings (78), zp-position-override/jog-pump-fill (66), + `gui.app` import and a 3-panel offscreen smoke (one edit → all three panels read `on / 45 % / "45%"`, one `M106 S115` on the wire). Pre-existing failures confirmed NOT from this change (reproduced with it stashed): `test_v75x_zp_dtr_no_reset::TestOpenStillConnects` ×2 and `test_v75x_zp_auto_reconnect_and_fast_z::test_no_z_max_keeps_defaults` (the documented 200-vs-500 `get_max_z_feedrate_mm_min` item). |
| `MEBP_v75x_CALIBRATION_USABILITY_POPUP.md` | v7.5.x new **"usability" pop-up communicating when XY / Z / P (pump) were last calibrated + recalibration warnings** (operator, changes.txt L22 — Cason 7.21). New per-machine **`SupportClasses/CalibrationStatusStore.py`** (`config/hardware/calibration_status.json`; `$MEBP_CALIBRATION_STATUS_DIR` override) owns a lifetime **XY travel odometer** (µm), per-type **`calibrated_at`** timestamps (xy/z/p; xy snapshots the odometer so travel-since-cal derives), and the two **thresholds** — `xy_recal_travel_mm` (**M**) + `recal_interval_hours` (**H**) — living here (not `HardwareConfig`) since only the pop-up reads them. **Odometer** = `PositionPoller.on_xy_travel`/`_accumulate_xy_travel` fires each poll-sample chord (catches ALL motion — jog + programmatic) → `StageController._note_xy_travel_um` → store (thread-safe + throttled ~15 s + `flush()` on shutdown); guards: `_last_odom_xy` reset on `set_stages`, jitter floor 1 µm, 1 m frame-discontinuity cap. **Timestamps** stamped where each calibration commits: **P** in `stage_panel._pump_setup_persist`, **Z** in `stage_panel._z_setup_capture_top` (apply_z_setup), **XY** in `calibration.py::_save_calibration` (change-signature gated so a Z-only save OR a calibration *load* — `_xy_cal_loaded` seed — doesn't bump XY). **UI:** "Recalibration Reminders" group (M/H spins + Save + **"Calibration status…"** button) on Hardware Setup → Device (`stage_panel._build_recal_reminders_group`). **Pop-up:** `gui/dialogs/calibration_status_dialog.py` (`evaluate_calibration_status` pure + `show_calibration_status(parent, force=)`); `app.py` startup trigger (`_maybe_show_calibration_status`, once/session, only-when-attention — clean setup never nags; `force=True` button always shows) + `save_settings` odometer flush. **Decisions (AskUserQuestion):** startup-only-when-needed + always-available button; ALL stage motion counts. **Needs real-HW/GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_calibration_usability_popup.py` (14) + offscreen panel/dialog smoke; last-known-calibration/jog-nav green (63). |
| `MEBP_v75x_ROSETTE_INK_PICKUP_WELL.md` | v7.5.x **fix — on a rosette plate the Quick Print ink pickup drove to the rosette PARENT centre instead of the assigned ink sub-well** (operator: "the needle goes to the center of the rosette not the ink rosette well"; buffer pickup was correct). **Root cause:** `ink_locations` (`ink→list[well]`) is **append-only** (`HardwareConfig.assign_wells_to_ink`, no plate pruning), so an ink pinned to `"A2"` *before* A2 became a rosette lingered as a stale index-`[0]` entry and adding the real sub-well gave `"Inert GFP": ["A2","A2.a"]`. `PlateDesign.compile()` **drops** the rosette parent (compiled plate has `A2.a/b/c`, no bare `"A2"`), but calibration re-adds the parent at the sub-well **centroid** in the `well_positions` map — and every reagent-pickup resolver took **`wells[0]`** = `"A2"` → dipped at the centroid `(87234,67824)`. Buffer worked only because its list was one real sub-well. (The Well-Setup *seed* path already validated vs `plate.well_names`, which excludes parents; the pickup resolvers validated vs `well_positions`, which includes the centroid — the asymmetry.) **Fix (all reagents + self-clean, operator-chosen):** new `_reagent_prep.resolve_pickup_well(wells, plate)` prefers a real leaf well in `plate.well_names` over a flattened parent (falls back to `[0]` → byte-identical legacy); `service_well_names`/`resolve_service_positions` gain a `plate=` param; applied at all pickup sites (`quick_print._ink_source_well` + multi-ink, `cell_targeting`/`cell_labeling._reagent_source_well`, spheroid's private `_service_well_names`) with `self._plate` threaded through every service call. **Data hygiene:** `HardwareConfig._drop_redundant_parents` (drop bare `"A2"` when `"A2.<x>"` is in the same list — plate-independent string rule) in `assign_wells_to_ink` + `_prune_ink_locations` (one-time `from_dict` migration `["A2","A2.a"]→["A2.a"]`); new `clear_all_ink_locations()`. **UI:** new **"Clear all wells"** button (confirmed `_loc_clear_all`) in Hardware Setup → Ink → Reagent Locations = a hard fresh-start reset that wipes stale/hidden entries. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V3** (ink aspirate lands on `A2.a`, not the centroid). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_rosette_ink_pickup_well.py` (16) + quick-print-pick-place/rosette-flatten/plate-types/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/spheroid/workflow-settings/ink-location-assignments+colors/multi-ink suites green (289). |

Name-keyed store paths → a future "Bright Field 6" needs only a `CHANNELS`/`CHANNEL_NUMBERS` entry. **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_fluorescence_mosaic.py` (35, +2) + common-print-settings/workflow-settings-popout/pump-settle-prime suites green (92). |

Advanced…) + `_ploc_mosaic_prev_col` (live-mosaic preview column → microscope feed gets full width for click-rim) and force-exits/hides the Manual-align group (its only entry point hides with the box). Shared widgets (banner — text swapped per mode via `_PLOC_BANNER_MOSAIC/_LEGACY`, plate view + Free/Snap, XZ view, live feed, Confirm/Skip/Cancel row — Cancel is shared with mosaic-scan cancel) stay. **Click gating:** plate-view clicks mutate the queue ONLY in legacy mode (gate sits AFTER the manual re-anchor stage routing so re-anchor overview clicks keep working in mosaic mode). **Run guard:** switching refused (combo reverts) while a queue run or mosaic scan is active. **Persisted** in settings.json `plate_location_prefs.workflow` (merge-preserving + `save()`), restored at build (missing/garbage → mosaic). **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_location_workflow_toggle.py` (21); plate-mosaic/rosette-auto-reanchor/single-well-reregister/click-rim/orientation-remap suites green (1 documented pre-existing CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_ABSTRACT_INKS_RETRACE_QUICKPRINT_SWAPS.md` | v7.5.x **four operator-requested Print Builder → Sketch changes (one landing).** **(1) Optimizer path flexibility — retrace along an existing bead:** instead of a pen-up, when the next shape starts a *brief length* back along an already-printed bead (same ink, same Z) the needle stays DOWN and retraces there; opt-in `Sketch.overlap_travel_enabled`/`_max_mm`/`_pause_pump`/`_speed_factor` (conditional serialize → legacy byte-identical when off) + `SketchShape.retrace_from` (optimizer-stamped). New `_retrace_polyline`; compiler tracks `prev_print_path` + emits the retrace (first-pass gated; `move_to(speed, pump_creep)`); optimizer post-pass `_stamp_segment_retraces`. **CRITICAL round-trip:** a PAUSED retrace advances the pump by a negligible `pump_creep` (floored `>1e-9`/segment) so Quick Print's `_travel_mask` (`dp<=1e-9 & moving`→travel) never re-splits it into a lift+pressure-relief — "pause not stop." **(2) Abstract inks (pump-agnostic sketch):** NEW `SketchInk{id,name,color}` + `Sketch.inks`/`_next_ink_id` (+`ensure_default_ink`/`ink_by_id`/`ink_order_index`/`add_ink`/`rebuild_inks_from_shapes`); `SketchShape.pump_index`→**`ink_id`** (legacy migrate `pump_index`→`ink_id`=pump+1, verified on real `config/prints/*.json`). The 3 trajectory pump columns become a PREVIEW artifact keyed by `ink_order_index%3`; the single-needle weld break keys on the TRUE `ink_id` (`prev_print_ink`) so mod-3-colliding inks still break; unlimited inks OK (sequential swaps = one ink at a time). New Sketch-page **Inks** manager card (add/rename/recolor/delete + reassign-on-delete) + per-shape **Ink** picker replacing P1/P2/P3. **(3) Quick-Print runtime ink mapping + full sequential swaps:** a sketch using ≥2 abstract inks gets a mapping table (abstract ink → configured ink→pump, colour-defaulted, validated) + a one-worker engine: `run_prep` once → per ink-group (`aspirate_ink` mapped pump → discrete `PrintManager` job the worker blocks on) → `run_post_clean` (waste→wash→buffer) between → `run_print_cleanup` once → end at safe Z; reuses existing `PickPlaceExecutor` primitives (NOT the legacy `PrintPlanExecutor`/`InkSwapStrategy`). `_ink_groups` recompiles per-group sub-sketches (preserves welds/retrace within a group). `_build_settings(pump=)`; `_PrintBridge.multi_done`; abort/button/status wired. Single-ink & non-sketch prints keep the legacy path. Deferred (v1): multi-group syringe-budget pre-flight + per-group flow-ceiling precision. **(4) Right context panel rework:** one outer `QScrollArea` + PINNED Save/Send; the sequence card drops its fixed-height nested scroll; each print section is a COLLAPSIBLE content-sized block (chevron + clickable title→select shapes + per-shape op rows) that grows to fit; per-section collapse persisted. **Designed via 2 Explore + 2 Plan agents; AskUserQuestion chose unlimited-inks / full-swaps / retrace-along-bead.** **Needs GUI/HW verification on ME3B V1** (assign abstract inks + panel colours/collapse; Overlap-travel retrace holds pressure with no relief; multi-ink Quick Print maps + swaps + ends at safe Z). Tests: `test_v75x_sketch_abstract_inks.py` (17) + `test_v75x_sketch_panel_rework.py` (4) + `test_v75x_sketch_overlap_travel.py` (18, incl. `_travel_mask` round-trip guard) + `test_v75x_quick_print_multi_ink.py` (15); sketch (257) + quick-print (137) + adjacent (83) suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ROSETTE_TAB_AUTO_REANCHOR_AND_MOSAIC_UI.md` | v7.5.x operator-feedback batch on plate calibration (follow-on to `SINGLE_WELL_MOSAIC_AND_GUIDED_MAPPING`). **BUG FIX — mapped centres offset by the registration shift:** `MosaicWellMappingDialog` back-projected clicks with the shift-INCLUDING `canvas_extent_um` (`MosaicBuilder._global_shift_um` — seeded from `MosaicAlignmentStore` and/or measured during stitching) while tile pixels sit at trusted raw stage positions; the detection path removes the shift (`_ploc_mosaic_world_shift`), the dialog didn't — so single-well/rosette sub-well AND full-plate "Map wells…" results were offset by it. Fix: `MosaicStore.save(shift_um=)` records the baked-in shift (+`get_shift_um`, legacy→(0,0); `update_extent` = metadata-only translate), all persist sites pass the live shift (captured BEFORE the builder is nulled in the finish path; `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` preserves it on re-save), and every mapping path hands the dialog **extent − shift** (zero shift ⇒ byte-identical, regression-locked). Pre-fix saved mosaics have unknown baked shifts → re-scan. **Plate mapping = MAIN wells only:** `main_well_names(plate)` (sub-wells excluded; dropped rosette parents re-added at their sub-well centroid via `_nominal_position`; `corner_well_names(names=, positions=)` overrides); `_autofill`/refine-queue/auto-detect grid use `_map_names`; sub-wells calibrate ONLY via the Rosettes flow. **Four-button Mosaic group** (operator: "too many buttons"): `Calibrate mosaic scan` · `Mosaic scan` (full-plate finish now **auto-opens** the Map-wells dialog) · `Re-anchor mosaic` · `Auto re-anchor mosaic`, + Plate-view combo + "Well scans" toggle + **Advanced… ▾ menu** (Settings/Map wells/Quick re-register/Scan well/Re-register from scanned (N)/Re-derive/Manual-align-toggle) — legacy button ATTRS stay alive as hidden buttons; menu actions `.click()` them and `_ploc_sync_advanced_menu` mirrors text+enabled on `aboutToShow` (all gating/count labels flow through). **NEW hands-free "Auto re-anchor mosaic":** the manual re-anchor's live click auto-saves a ~¼-frame feature patch + its patch-centre abs µm + capture µm/px (`SupportClasses/ReanchorFeatureStore.py`, per plate key; position kept in the MAP frame — shifted by every `_ploc_apply_global_translation` and warped by the 2+ re-register); `_ploc_auto_reanchor` → `_AutoReanchorWorker(QThread)` (poller-suspended, `safe_travel_to` the stored position retract-first, `capture_fresh_frame`, patch rescaled by stored/live µm/px, `VisionDetector.find_template` TM_CCOEFF_NORMED conf-gate 0.5, rotation-aware `pixel_to_stage_offset`) → GUI applies `E = actual − stored` via the shared translation (mosaic+wells+feature move together). Gates: XY, Safe-Z-when-ZP, camera+µm/px, feature saved, map exists. **New "Rosettes" tab** (index 3, after Plate Location; `_zauto_tab_index` 3→4 — only hard-coded index): rosette-well picker ("(scanned)" annotated) + "Scan rosette well" (`_ploc_scan_single_well(name, True)` — factored out of `_ploc_scan_well`) + **"Map sub-wells from saved scan…"** (re-opens the mapping from the persisted `plate#well` mosaic + stored shift/µm-px — no re-scan/motion) + status + own live feed started by `_on_workflow_tab_changed`. **Single-well mosaics overlay the plate mosaic:** `MosaicStore.composite_with_wells` pastes each `plate#well` image at its extent (proportional crop when clipped); "Well scans" toggles (default ON) on Plate Location + Jog; **purity guard** — `_ploc_overlay_img` stays the PLAIN plate image (what `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` re-persists), the composite exists only in the pushed pixmap; `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` also translates every well-entry extent (metadata-only) so everything moves as one. **"Mosaic + well" = circles only:** `JogWorkspaceView` overlay mode draws NO plate body and outline-only rings (green cal / yellow approx / blue current) for every entry INCLUDING sub-wells (`_well_radius_um(name)` resolves per-well diameters); "well" mode byte-identical. Implemented alongside a concurrent session's calibration.py edits (Pump Compliance tab) — reconciled. **Needs real-HW/GUI verification on ME3B V1** (scan→auto-mapping main-wells-only lands correctly; Rosettes tab sub-wells land correctly — the shift bug fix; manual re-anchor saves the feature → auto re-anchor travels/matches/shifts; well-scan overlay + circles-only view). Pre-addendum mosaics have no stored mapping — run Map wells… once to seed. |

| `MEBP_v75x_WELL_TYPE_PRESETS.md` | v7.5.x **well-type presets for rosette wells** (operator: when adding a well to a rosette, pick a standard vessel — e.g. a **0.1 mL PCR tube** — instead of typing raw geometry; build out custom presets over time). The needle-clearance mechanism the request describes ALREADY exists (v7.4.8): a sub-well's **rim height** (how far the tube top sits above the plate) flows `Well.rim_height_mm` → `WellPlate.max_rim_height_mm` → `app.py::_update_insert_clearance` (`plate_top_z + max_rim + 3 mm margin`) → `StageController.set_min_travel_z` → floors every `safe_travel_to`/`ensure_retracted_to` retract (polarity-safe) so the needle clears the tallest tube before travelling over/into it — **that safety path is unchanged**. This adds a preset **library** to set that geometry in one click. **Decisions (operator):** full preset library (builtin + user-savable, builtin/user shadow-by-id) · single opening diameter (taper deferred) · reuse the existing rim-above-top semantics (no clearance-math change). **New `SupportClasses/WellTypeStore.py`** — pure-JSON `WellType` (id, display_name, `diameter_mm`, `well_depth_mm`, `rim_height_mm`, optional `ink_z_mm`/`volume_uL`, `builtin`) + `WellTypeStore` (`reload`/`get`/`all`/`save_user`/`delete_user`, atomic writes, lazy `get_store()` singleton), a close mirror of `PlateTypeStore`; dirs `config/hardware/well_types/{builtin,user}` (user shadows builtin by id). Ships **`builtin/pcr-tube-0.1ml.json`** (Ø 6, depth 20, rim 20, 100 µL; rim is a tunable guess). **Model:** `PlateDesign.Well.well_type_id: Optional[str]` (design-time link to the preset), conditional-serialized in `_entity_to_dict` (emit only when set → byte-identical legacy) + read in `_entity_from_dict`; `WellInfo`/`compile()` untouched (runtime uses the stamped geometry). **GUI** (`gui/pages/hardware/plate_designer.py`, `_build_well_card` rosette block, `_edit_context is not None`): a **Well type** `QComboBox` (`(custom)` + all types) + **Save as well type…** + **Delete** (user types only); handlers `_apply_well_type` (stamps diameter/depth/rim, and ink_z only when the type prescribes one; `None`=custom clears the link keeping geometry; scene redraw + `QTimer.singleShot(0, …)` deferred panel rebuild to avoid deleting the combo mid-signal), `_save_current_as_well_type`, `_delete_selected_well_type`. **Needs real-HW/GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_well_type_presets.py` (18); rosette-flatten/plate-types/plate-designer regression (80) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_OVERLAP_SEQUENCE_AND_NEEDLE_MODE.md` | v7.5.x three operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions. **(A) Closed-loop over-closure toggle:** `SketchShape.overlap_closure` (per-shape, closed outlines only; conditional serialize → legacy-identical) — the compiler continues the printed path PAST the seam by ~the needle **outer radius** (`od/2`, ½-bead fallback) via new `extend_closed_path(path, overshoot)` (appended per pass AFTER the weld-flip; ≤ one lap; never re-closes — intentionally ends past the seam), so the loop fully closes (otherwise the needle pushes ink aside on re-entry and it doesn't close). No-op on open/filled shapes. **(B) Print-sequence panel:** new `plan_print_sections(sketch, needle, single_needle)` returns the ordered list of colour-coded continuous **sections** (maximal runs printed as one bead) + the **moves** between them, MIRRORING the compiler grouping exactly (first-pass weld + far-end flip; last-pass end + overshoot as the exit; `break_before` = `move`/`ink_change`/`layer`); groups by OBJECT (layer 0). A persistent right-panel card renders it (section rows coloured by the pump→ink from `_channel_info` built from `config.pumps`, click→`canvas.select_indices`; break rows; summary). **(C) Single- vs multi-needle mode:** `Sketch.single_needle` (tri-state: None=AUTO→`needle.num_channels<=1`, else explicit; `is_single_needle(needle)`). Operator-chosen semantics (AskUserQuestion): a channel/ink change breaks the bead **only in single-needle mode** (the compiler tracks `prev_print_pump` — set on printing passes only — and refuses to weld across a channel change; move-only `no_print` passes never gate); multi keeps channels welded (legacy). `optimize_print_order` gained `needle`/`single_needle` (single mode groups same-channel shapes to cut ink swaps). Auto-detect checkbox on the sequence card, overridable. **Panel==compiled trajectory in both modes.** Latent fix: the page iterated `config.pumps` as a list (it's a dict) → never found a syringe; now dict-safe. **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → skeptic verify: 5 confirmed / 1 rejected): fixed `prev_print_pump` set by move-only passes (HIGH), plan endpoint ignoring overlap overshoot (HIGH) + thick-outline last-pass end (MEDIUM); documented intra-object multi-pass lifts + odd-layer overlap direction as intentional layer-0/per-object approximations; rejected "extend_closed_path doesn't re-close" (intentional). **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_overlap_sequence_needle_mode.py` (31); sketch + print-library/seam/quick-print/extrusion suites green (288). |
| `MEBP_v75x_CONTEXT_PANEL_PILLS_AND_CUSTOM_SECTIONS.md` | v7.5.x **left context panel becomes a pill-picker host + a composable Custom panel, and the "jog panel sometimes disappears in a workflow" bug is fixed** (operator). **Bug root cause:** the left box's **visibility** (`_navigate_to` → `UIFunctions.toggleLeftBox`, an *animation toggle* keyed on `isVisible()`) and its **content** (`_update_mode_context` from `sub_page_changed`, which never touched visibility) were decided by two paths that don't both run on every transition; `WorkflowsModePage` isn't a `ModePage`, so `_navigate_to` pointed the stack at a *placeholder* and the jog panel only mounted on a later `sub_page_changed` → order-dependent shown-but-empty / content-but-hidden. **Fix:** a **single global `ContextPanelHost`** (`gui/widgets/context_panel_host.py`) replaces the per-page left `_context_stack`, and **one `MainWindow._refresh_left_context()`** (called from BOTH `_navigate_to` and `_on_mode_sub_page_changed`; `_update_mode_context` deleted) mounts the current page's `get_context_widget()` in the host's native slot (reused `QScrollArea` wrappers keyed by widget id — never `deleteLater`) AND sets visibility **explicitly** (no toggle; exception in a delegate → treated as no-native, never vanishes). **Feature (operator chose view-picker + shared scope):** the host has a `SegmentedPillBar` — **(Jog)** = the page's native context widget, **(Custom)** = a shared `CustomContextPanel` (`gui/widgets/custom_context_panel.py`) the operator composes from **sections/cards** via `＋ Add section`: **live camera** (`CameraFeedView`; safe-start on show, never stops the shared feed), **syringe overview** (`PumpRack`, fill-dict from `jog_control._refresh_pump_panel`; ink accent `.color`), **positions** (new light `PositionReadoutCard` reusing `PositionBar` + `_update_position_displays` read logic; Z→user frame, pump→µL), **jog** (`StandardJogContextPanel`), **hardware info** — registry in `gui/widgets/context_sections.py` (`SectionContext` + `register_section`/`catalog`/`build_section`; each best-effort → a broken builder shows an error card). Layout is a **single shared, persisted, machine-level** thing — `SupportClasses/ContextPanelLayoutStore.py` (`config/context_panel_layout.json`; atomic tmp+`os.replace`, listeners, env `MEBP_CONTEXT_PANEL_DIR`, `known_types` prune) → same cards everywhere the box appears, survives restart. Live `on_status_update`/`on_motion_tick` forwarded app → host → Custom sections (native jog panel stays page-ticked → no double-drive). **Scope:** `in_scope = native context is not None` = exactly "everywhere the left box already appears" (Jog / Calibration / jog-capable Workflow tiles); Print Builder / Full Print inner / Fluorescence / Common Print Settings / picker return `None` and keep no box (unchanged) — so the `full_print` carve-out / `current_workflow_id()` from the draft plan proved unnecessary. Host persists the **requested** pill (`context_panel.active_view`) distinct from the *displayed* view so an availability-driven fallback can't clobber a saved "Jog" at startup; `context_panel.collapsed` remembers manual collapse. Close-x + top-bar toggle → `_toggle_left_context`; host added to `_propagate_hardware_config` fan-out. **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_context_panel.py` (20; incl. `_refresh_left_context` invoked unbound against a fake `self` — full MainWindow boot is intractable headless); full-print/jog-nav/needle-location/plate-z-autocal/plate-location baselines green (121). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_START_POINT_AND_PATH_OPTIMIZE.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions for **print continuity** ("a shape is placed down and the start/stop location is standard … not good for print continuity"). **(1) Manual per-shape print start/stop with snap-to-lines:** new `SketchShape.start_point: tuple\|None` (conditional serialize → byte-identical legacy when `None`) — a world-mm anchor for WHERE a shape begins printing. Compiler helper `reorder_path_to_start(path, start_xy, closed)` (open → reverse to the nearer endpoint; closed → roll the ring to the nearest vertex + re-close; deposited geometry unchanged, only the entry/exit/seam moves); `_shape_paths` is now a thin wrapper over `_shape_paths_raw` that reorders each **unfilled outline** pass to the start point (fills / `region` / `travel` never reordered). Canvas gains a **draggable green start marker** (`_effective_start_world`/`_resolve_start_on_shape`/`_start_marker_screen`/`_start_handle_at`/`_draw_start_marker`; offset outward from the shape so it never collides with the blue resize handles; hit-tested BEFORE resize handles); dragging it reuses the existing `_snap(raw, exclude=selected)` → **snaps to existing lines/vertices**, stored via a new `_apply_resize` `"start"` case; `clear_start_point`. Page shows a "Print start: custom/default" row + hint + "Reset start point" for outline shapes. A shape can now start exactly where the previous one ended → the existing compiler **weld** machinery fuses them (no pen-up). **(2) Auto path-optimize postprocess (operator chose FULL reorder):** `optimize_print_order(sketch)` (pure, returns a copy) partitions the shape list at user-placed `travel` retract points (**fixed breaks**) and per segment runs a **greedy nearest-neighbour with all-seed restarts** (≤ 14 shapes), choosing each shape's entry orientation (rotate loop / flip line) and stamping `start_point` to chain them into the fewest pen-up travels; preserves all print params. Canvas `optimize()` (undoable) + a "✨ Optimize print path" button on the Print-parameters card reporting "N → M travel(s)" via `count_discontinuities` (counts lift-to-max-Z groups − 1 — the `n_lifts` ground truth; immune to the zero-length coincident weld-node segment that fooled a first pump-flat-run metric). **Fills/regions kept fixed** in the optimizer v1 (ordered, not reoriented). Design-time only (no motion); embedded vector `Sketch` (Library "Edit in Sketch") round-trips the new field. **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_start_point_and_optimize.py` (33); existing sketch suites (154) + print-library/seam/quick-print-travel (40) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_REANCHOR_MOSAIC_AND_CAMERA_ORIENTATION.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested plate-calibration changes. **(1) Re-anchor moves the mosaic + wells as ONE unit, no-travel.** The mosaic is scanned once per plate TYPE; when the plate is remounted the whole map is offset. "Re-anchor map" previously shifted only the **well centres**, leaving the **mosaic image** behind. Now `CalibrationPage._ploc_apply_global_translation` also calls new `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by(ex,ey)` (extracted from `_ploc_store_manual_align`, which is refactored to reuse it) — shifts the cached overlay extent, persists to `MosaicStore` (before the `calibration_data_changed` emit → the Jog page re-reads the shifted extent), re-pushes via `_ploc_set_overlay_image` (idempotent: resets the view user-shift + align sliders). Re-anchor is now **NO-TRAVEL**: `_ploc_reanchor_live_click` (step 1) captures the feature's true absolute µm from the LIVE click (`get_xy_position(cached=False)` + `pixel_to_stage_offset`), `_ploc_reanchor_overview` (step 2) reads the MAP position from an overview click (well-snap `_predict_well_xy` or Free-select zero-ref+zero), `E = live − map` (same sign as the old `feature_abs − target`), `_ploc_safe_goto` NO LONGER called, Safe-Z gate dropped (no motion); overview click handlers gate on `_ploc_reanchor_stage` so a stray click can't pollute the run queue. Robust to an arbitrarily large remount offset (the old travel-first flow only worked when the offset was < ~½ FOV). **(2) Calibrated camera-vs-stage rotation applied to the click mapping.** `CameraManager.pixel_to_stage_offset` ignored the camera's mount rotation (identity: image-right=+X, down=+Y) → a rotated microscope camera drove live-view clicks the wrong way. It now applies the calibrated `rotation_deg` as `(dx_um,dy_um)=µm/px·R(θ)·(dx_px,dy_px)` (standard CCW `R(θ)`); **at θ=0/None it reduces EXACTLY to the legacy identity** (no regression for aligned/uncalibrated cameras). The sign is DERIVED from `PixelCalibrationDialog.plus_column_direction_deg`'s documented model (`p=−scale·R(α)·m`, stored `θ=−α`, so click→stage inverse is `µm/px·R(θ)·p`) and locked by a round-trip test. **Only the click mapping changes** — the displayed feed, the raw frames, and the `MosaicBuilder` build (which places tiles by stage position with NO per-frame rotation, so a capture-level flip would scramble it) are all untouched; changing orientation does NOT invalidate a scanned mosaic. Reuses the existing measurement/storage/restore infra: a new **"Calibrate orientation (vs stage)…"** button + "Camera rotation vs stage: θ°" readout on the `objective_calibration_card` measures rotation via `PixelCalibrationDialog` (no µm/px recal), pushes `set_rotation_deg` live, persists per camera identity via new `CameraCalibrationStore.set_rotation`/`get_rotation` (rotation-only write preserving `um_per_px`/`image_correction`/`hw_controls` siblings) + syncs the fresh rotation into EVERY objective with a µm/px cal (mount rotation is a camera property, not per-objective); `hardware_setup._restore_calibration_for_slot` now restores `rotation_deg` **independent of µm/px**. **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → independent verify): rotation-math + re-anchor-flow clean; 3 confirmed defects fixed — malformed-extent guard in `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` (IndexError → logged no-op), the all-objectives rotation sync above (an objective swap could push a STALE per-objective rotation over the fresh one), and pre-existing `ObjectiveCalibrationStore.set_calibration` no longer drops a stored `rotation_deg` on a µm/px-only update. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (calibrate orientation → plausible angle; live-view click maps the right way — else flip the one-line `R(θ)` sign; re-anchor a remounted plate → mosaic + wells shift together on Plate Location + Jog, no stage motion, persists on restart). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_reanchor_mosaic_and_camera_orientation.py` (21, incl. the sign-lock round-trip); objective-cal/camera-rotation/camera-cal-store (65) + image-correction/mosaic-orientation-remap/plate-orientation/plate-location-click-rim green; plate-mosaic 115/116 (the 1 fail is the documented pre-existing CV `test_real_24_well_mosaic`). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_RETRACT_POINT_AND_BACKTRACE.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions. **(1) Colour-coded retract-&-move point** ("a color coded point … that retracts and moves the needle"): new `"travel"` shape kind — a placeable pen-up marker (pink diamond + up-chevron, `Tool.TRAVEL`, `TRAVEL_HEX`). The compiler emits `lift_out(z_travel)` → move to its `cx,cy` at the retracted travel Z (deposits nothing, stays up); it **splits the toolpath into runs**. **(2) Back-trace any path** ("take the continuous collection of lines between the retract points and move the needle back through with a user defined offset" + follow-up "also … in the plane"): retrace a run **reversed**, offset in **Z-height** AND **in-plane** (perpendicular parallel curve), with an **extrude-on-return toggle** (2nd bead / move-only). **Model:** `SketchShape` gains `z_offset_mm` (per-shape print-height offset) + `no_print` (move-only), both serialized (defaults → byte-identical legacy). **Compiler:** per-shape print Z = `max(0, layer_z + z_offset_mm)` (floored at the plate bottom so a negative offset can't punch through), `do_print = not no_print`, `z_travel` clears the tallest offset. New module fns `offset_polyline(pts, distance, closed)` (per-vertex averaged-normal parallel offset, consecutive-duplicate-safe, clamped miter) + `backtrace_shape(shape, *, z_offset, xy_offset, print_on_return)` (reversed + offset copy; lines/polys/regions reverse points + in-plane offset, circle/ellipse/rect grow by the offset; accumulated Z clamped ±40 mm so chained back-traces can't run away). **Canvas** (`sketch_canvas.py`): `_place_travel`, `_run_indices(seed)` (maximal printing run bounded by travel points), `backtrace_run(seed, z_off, xy_off, print_on_return)` inserts reversed copies right after the run; travel cases in draw/hit/extent/move/transform/snap; `fit_view` scale clamped to 200 (tiny-span guard). **Page** (`print_builder_sketch.py`): retract tool button, travel-point props card, `_build_backtrace_card` (Z / in-plane offset spins + "Extrude on return" checkbox + button) shown for a single non-travel selection; `_backtrace_selected` confirms on a negative Z offset (collision nudge). **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → verify, 21/38 confirmed): 6 fixes (chained-Z clamp, offset-polyline dedup, negative-Z confirm, fit-view zoom clamp, explicit travel no-ops); two HIGH flags proven non-bugs + locked with tests (multi-layer serpentine only flips travel *direction* not deposited geometry; travel-as-first-shape starts retracted at z_travel, and the `build_well_plate_job` preamble retracts anyway). **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_retract_and_backtrace.py` (43); sketch/print-library/quick-print/multi-object-seam/print-setup-routine suites green (193). |
| `MEBP_v75x_EXTRUSION_VOLUME_CALC_AUDIT.md` | v7.5.x **audit + fixes for "27 G needs ~7× extrusion to print anything"** (operator asked for a full print-volume calculation check). **Verdict: the core arithmetic is correct — no units bug / hidden factor.** The whole Quick Print chain deposits exactly `bore_area × modifier` µL per mm (`flow = pct·area·xy_max·mod`, `speed = pct·xy_max` → `vol/mm = flow/speed = area·mod`; `move_pump_uL` µL→plunger-mm is clean; the Hagen–Poiseuille flow ceiling for a 27 G at water is ~376 µL/s, far above any print flow, so the clamp isn't limiting). Needing a multiplier is BY DESIGN — the 1× model = a filament the exact diameter of the bore = the theoretical minimum; a visible/spreading bead + prime + compliance need more. Four findings compounded it; operator chose to fix **A + C + E** (D folded into A). **(C) drop-gate under-extrusion (real defect):** `PrintManager._execute_print_path` skipped any segment whose `volume_uL ≤ 0.001` (at 27 G 1× = every segment < 0.029 mm) and `ZPStage.move_relative` drops `<1e-4 mm` plunger deltas — NEITHER accumulated → finely-sampled paths lost volume, worst at low extrusion (→ operator over-cranks the modifier). Fix: accumulate a `_pending_pump_uL` residual across segments, emit once it crosses `_PATH_PUMP_EMIT_MIN_UL` (0.001 µL), flush the tail at path end; pacing/logging still use the nominal per-segment volume (timing unchanged). **(A) two inconsistent bead models unified:** the Sketch compiler used `id_mm × layer_height × mult` (rectangle, layer-height-dependent) while FlowPhysics/GeometryEngine/Quick Print use `π·(id/2)² × mult` (bore area) — CLAUDE.md F-1 claimed "ONE bead model everywhere" but Sketch was never migrated. `SketchTrajectory.compile_to_trajectory` now uses the canonical **bore-area × mult** when a needle is supplied (falls back to `bead × layer_height × mult` only when no needle); its shaded thickness band stays a display WIDTH (`id × mult`) — width vs area differ by design, same as Quick Print. **(D) stale GeometryEngine module docstring** ("flow_rate = travel_speed × needle_OD × layer_height") updated to the bore-area model. **(E) modifier had no effect on the parametric/trajectory path:** `PrintObject.extrusion_modifier` existed but `generate_object_trajectory` never read it (called `compute_pump_positions`/`compute_total_volume` at 1.0) and there was no UI → Print Builder parametric objects / Full Print ran locked at 1×. Fix: `generate_object_trajectory` reads `obj.extrusion_modifier` and threads it through the 11 internal builders → the volume functions (all new params default 1.0 = byte-identical legacy; explicit `outer_ring_volume_uL` stays an absolute target since the modifier cancels in its ratio); a new **"Extrusion ×"** spin in the Print Builder object designer captured into `params["extrusion_modifier"]`, lifted onto the field in `_build_print_object` + the collection-commit `PrintObject`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (re-tune the 27 G multiplier — fine-path drops fixed + parametric path now honors the modifier, so it should need a smaller value). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_extrusion_volume_calc.py` (14) + updated `test_v75x_sketch_extrusion_thickness.py`; extrusion/sketch/quick-print/seam/planner-barrier/print-setup/full-print/library suites green (294). Pre-existing unrelated failures: `test_v73_trajectory_planner.TestFullPlanGeneration` (6, MagicMock `<` in `PrintTrajectoryPlanner._move_z`, untouched by this change). |
| `MEBP_v75x_JOG_TRAVEL_OFF_GUI_THREAD.md` | v7.5.x **fix — "program freezes when I jog to another well with the needle DOWN, but not if I hit Move-to-Safe-Z first"** (operator; suspected a safety-check bug). **Root cause (4-lens investigation + adversarial verify + `logs/app.log`):** NOT a broken safety check and NOT a deadlock — the Jog page's click-to-travel handlers called `StageController.safe_travel_to(...)` **synchronously on the Qt GUI thread**; step-1 Z-retract-and-confirm (`flush_moves` M400 + `wait_for_z_arrival`) takes **11–14 s** on ME3B V1 (up to ~60–120 s on a stall), freezing the event loop the whole time. "Move to Safe Z" first is fire-and-forget (`move_z_absolute` returns instantly), so the subsequent click's step-1 retract is a fast no-op → no freeze. The workflow pages (Quick Print pre-position, pick-&-place executors) already ran `safe_travel_to` off-thread; the Jog page + the workflow-page CLICK handlers were the outliers. **Fix (operator chose full + serial hardening):** new reusable `gui/widgets/safe_travel_worker.py::SafeTravelWorker(QObject)` runs `safe_travel_to` on a daemon thread, marshals `finished(bool)` back via a queued Signal, **busy-guard** so re-clicks are ignored (not queued behind each other on the shared serial channel). Wired into all 5 click sites: `jog_control` (workspace click + right-click fast-travel; + WaitCursor cue), `standard_jog_context._absolute_goto` (Absolute Go-To; Go button → "Travelling…"), and `spheroid_pickup`/`cell_targeting`/`cell_labeling` (both click handlers). Retract-before-XY safety **unchanged** (same `safe_travel_to` args; only *where the blocking wait runs* changed). **Serial hardening:** `ZPStage._read_until_ok` gains an absolute wall-clock cap `READ_OK_HARD_CAP_S=180 s` (`hard_deadline = now + max(ok_timeout, cap)`, top-of-loop check → outcome `hard_timeout`) so a never-completing move — whose Marlin `busy` keep-alives used to reset the wait window forever — fails after a generous ceiling instead of hanging the worker thread (and every ZP thread behind `_serial_lock`) indefinitely; `flush_moves` (own bounded loop) unaffected. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_jog_travel_off_gui_thread.py` (8) + updated `test_v75x_z_retract_before_xy_travel.py` (join the worker thread); jog-nav/workflow-settings/ZP-serial/gentle-Z/safe-Z/spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/stress green. Pre-existing unrelated failure: `test_v75x_zp_auto_reconnect_and_fast_z::test_no_z_max_keeps_defaults` (uncommitted `get_max_z_feedrate_mm_min` WIP in StageController.py, not this change). |
