| `MEBP_v75x_SPHEROID_DISENGAGE_AND_SINK_TIMING.md` | v7.5.x **Spheroid Pick & Place: optional disengagement extra-aspirate + calibrated sink-timing model + guided calibration** (operator: a spheroid rises up the bore then SINKS; if it sinks before the needle leaves the well it's lost, if aspirated too far up it gets stuck, and at placement they want it just at the tip for minimal excess; also sometimes needs extra flow to pop it off the glass). **Model:** `height_mm = V_uL / bore_area_mm2` (bore = `NeedleSpec.cross_section_area_mm2`). Calibration builds a **timing curve `t_sink(lift)`** via a **cumulative staircase** (aspirate increasing volumes on one test spheroid; operator clicks "Visible at tip" each time it sinks back → sample `(lift=ΔV/area, sink_s)`). At run time (per move) the executor **inverts** the curve so `sink_time ≈ travel_time + margin`, sizing the aspirate to `max(carrier, timing_vol)` (**floor at the sphere-capture volume**) and waiting only the leftover at the destination — the spheroid stays lifted through the in-well retract, isn't lifted more than needed, and arrives at the tip for a **small release dispense** (needle keeps the rest; Post-clean clears the accumulation). **Disengagement** = an extra held aspirate applied as the **fast leading portion** of the total. **New:** `SupportClasses/SpheroidSinkCalibrationStore.py` (atomic-JSON singleton + standalone `SinkCurve` with monotone/clamped `lift_for_time`/`time_for_lift`; needle-independent — volume↔lift uses the *current* bore area); `SpheroidPickupConfig` fields (`disengage_enabled/volume_uL/rate_uL_s`, `sink_timing_enabled`, `travel_margin_s`, `release_enabled/volume_uL`) + `to_dict`; `PickPlaceExecutor` gains `bore_area_mm2`/`sink_curve` + `_estimate_travel_time_s` (XY dist ÷ measured/limit XY speed + Z retract/lower + overhead; div-0 safe) + `_planned_aspirate_uL`; `_execute_spheroid_pickup` rewritten. **GUI** (`spheroid_pickup_workflow.py`): "Sink timing & disengage (optional)" settings section + `_current_config`/`_on_start` wiring & gates (needle Ø + calibrated curve required when sink timing on) + `_bore_area_mm2()` + calibration-status label + `hideEvent` hook. **New dialog** `gui/pages/workflows/spheroid_sink_calibration.py::SinkDisengageCalibrationDialog` (modeless; live microscope; worker/bridge; staircase + disengage-test panels; every worker/Abort/hide ends at Safe Z; ZP-connected/Safe-Z/plate-bottom/bore-area preflight gates). **All new behavior OFF by default → byte-identical legacy sequence.** Aspirate=`−`/dispense=`+`, spheroid moves keep `relieve=False`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_spheroid_disengage_and_sink_timing.py` (21); spheroid-pick-place-z / workflow-settings-popout / pump-settle / cell-targeting / cell-labeling / picker-scaling suites green (142+). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_ENVELOPE_RESTORE_FROM_SETUP.md` | v7.5.x **fix — on restart a calibrated pump's LOCATION restores but its soft-limit MIN/MAX do not** (operator). On ME3B V3, P2 came up `[0, 30]` instead of the calibrated `[-30, 0]`. **Root cause:** the envelope had two persisted sources that drifted — `device_profile.pump_setup` (captured raw extremes, authoritative: P2 `raw_dispensed=0, raw_aspirated=-30` ⇒ `[-30,0]`) vs the separate `safety_limits.p2_min/max` mirror (stale, sign-flipped `[0,30]`). At startup `apply_pump_convention` restored the **datum + direction sign** from `pump_setup` but **not the envelope** (deferred to "the safety_limits section separately"), and `set_hardware_config`'s `update_from_hardware_config(skip_pumps=[…])` clobber-guard then **preserved** the stale mirror (it only blocks the syringe-stroke estimate; it never re-derives the calibrated envelope) → calibration owned datum+direction but not the envelope it captured. Secondary re-clobber: `app.py::_apply_and_save_after_restore` mirrors persisted `safety_limits` back into the live envelope on restore-prompt accept. **Fix:** `StageController.apply_pump_convention` now **re-derives the soft-limit envelope from the captured extremes** (`lo,hi = min/max(rd,ra)`, exact extremes/no margin, matching `apply_pump_setup`) so datum+direction+envelope all come from the one authoritative `pump_setup` on every boot (self-heals future drift); plus corrected the stale on-disk `safety_limits.p2` `[0,30]`→`[-30,0]` in `settings.json` + `config/hardware/devices/ME3B V3.json` so every source agrees (closes the `_apply_and_save_after_restore` hole). Uncalibrated pumps (no `pump_setup`) unaffected — still recomputed from the syringe stroke each boot. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V3.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_plunger_setup.py` (25, +`test_envelope_restored_overrides_stale_mirror` + envelope assertion in `test_restore_sign_and_datum`); pump-plunger/common-axis-speed/zp-envelope-absolute/jog-pump-fill suites green (62). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_WELL_AUTO_DETECTION.md` | v7.5.x **model-driven well auto-detection on a plate mosaic** (operator, 2026-07-30: *"detect 24 wells on the mosaic and do the best effort to ensure that they are where they should be and they are the size they should be … lets use that for the auto detection of wells button … we may have different parameters for a black bottom glass plate, or other well plate types … then we will make a nice classifier"*). **The button never worked on this plate:** `MosaicWellMappingDialog._auto_detect` called `WellDetector.detect_filled_wells` **and nothing else** — a generic blob finder, while on a clear plastic plate in brightfield the well is **a hole, not a bright blob** (interior and plate body read the same grey; only the moulded rim is bright) → measured **0 detections** on the operator's own mosaic, so the button reported "couldn't find the grid" every time on exactly the plate being scanned (the scan path only fared better because it falls through to Hough at <3; the dialog had no fallback). Neither used the fact that **the answer's shape is already known**: the plate definition gives 4×6 wells 19.3 mm apart 15.6 mm across and the mosaic carries its own px/µm, so the well radius and lattice spacing in PIXELS are known to ~1 % before looking at a pixel. **NEW GUI-free `SupportClasses/PlateWellDetector.py`**, four stages: **(1)** `ring_response` = matched filter with an annulus kernel (−1 just inside the known radius, +1 just outside), each lobe normalised to unit weight so it is a **difference of local means** — invariant to a stitched mosaic's tile-to-tile brightness steps; on the real plate the 24 wells score **22.8–45.0 while the best spurious peak scores 8.8** (2.6× gap), 0.02 s at ¼ res. **(2)** `fit_lattice` fits rows×cols as **rotation + per-axis scale + translation, deliberately NO shear** (a moulded plate cannot shear, and allowing it lets a wrong assignment hide in a skew), rotation seeded from a 90°-wedge angle histogram + a coarse sweep and translation found by **voting** (every (candidate,node) pairing implies one offset; the true one is voted for by every well at once) — which both labels the detections AND **manufactures the missing ones**, so the result always carries exactly rows×cols wells. **(3)** `refine_ring` walks a radial profile along 180 rays, takes the strongest correctly-signed sub-pixel step per ray, drops sectors that disagree (label text, bubbles, a neighbour's rim) and circle-fits the survivors — it MEASURES the well instead of assuming the catalogue size. **(4)** plate-level **regularisation**: a radius that is a MAD outlier or a centre that wandered off the lattice falls back to the plate median / lattice prediction (a moulded plate's wells are identical to well under a percent, so the median of 24 beats any lone disagreeing fit) — the "best effort to ensure they are where they should be" half. **🐞 TWO FINDINGS THAT SHAPED THE CODE: (a) inlier COUNT cannot score the lattice fit** — it saturates at "all wells" for a whole family of wrong fits (a 5° tilt still lands every well inside a 0.25-pitch gate), and a first cut scoring by count settled on a **−4.99° tilt with 6 % anisotropic pitch** and dragged the refinement with it; rotation is now chosen by **minimising the inlier residual**, count only ranks the discrete hypotheses. **(b) a WIDE radius band is actively harmful** — at ±30 % the radial walk on a low-contrast plate latched onto a shoulder outside the well and reported **18.38 mm for a 15.6 mm well**, where ±10 % measures **15.58 mm** on the same image; `diameter_tol` 0.30→**0.10** and `ring_band_frac` 0.10→**0.06**, both picked by a sweep over the three real mosaics. **Refusing beats guessing** (placing a full grid of wrong markers is worse than "I couldn't find it"; the 3-corner manual teach stays armed on every refusal): **lattice-inlier fraction ≥ 0.60** catches a wrong plate type — a 96-well grid on the 24-well mosaic still "measures" **79 %** of its nodes (the walk finds *some* edge inside those big wells) but only **11 %** are real rings — and 0.60 not 0.50 because a square grid also admits a **45° lattice at √2× the pitch** that lands on exactly half the wells; **measured fraction ≥ 0.25** catches a wrong appearance profile (forcing the wrong polarity still snaps half the lattice but measures nothing). A **180°-flipped lattice fits equally well** (the plate is symmetric — nothing in the IMAGE says which corner is A1) and the two solutions put row/col labels on opposite corners, so the fit is **canonicalised to the near-0° solution**; which corner is really A1 stays the caller's decision from the plate-orientation convention, not from pixels. **PER-PLATE-TYPE PARAMETERS = the path to the classifier:** everything appearance-dependent lives in `WellAppearance`, carried on `PlateType.well_detection` (conditional-serialised → every existing plate type round-trips byte-identically); a black-bottom glass plate needs **a different edge polarity, not different code**. `edge_polarity="auto"` (the default) runs both polarities and keeps whichever locks the lattice better — so a brand-new product works untuned — and **reports the winner** so it can be stored; presets `clear_plastic_rim`/`bright_disc`/`dark_disc`. Those stored profiles ARE the labelled training set a classifier would need, produced as a by-product of ordinary use. `nest-plastic-24.json` stamped `clear_plastic_rim` now the operator's scan settled it (deterministic, 0.38 s vs 0.51 s). **⭐ RESULT on the operator's mosaic (3000×1944, 1410 tiles), 0.4 s: 24/24 wells, ALL measured (none filled from the grid), Ø 15.63 mm vs 15.60 nominal (+0.2 %), well-to-well radius σ = 0.95 px = 38 µm, rotation −0.30°, centre-vs-lattice RMS 3.5 px = 140 µm** — every ring sits on the inner edge of the bright moulded rim. **A free cross-check falls out:** the plate is a manufactured ruler, so a systematic pitch error is evidence about the **mosaic's µm/px** — measured 19.14 mm vs 19.30 nominal says this mosaic's scale is ~**0.9 % small**, and it warns (the older `24` mosaic measures 19.32/19.40 — that one's scale is right). Other mosaics: `24` 23/24 + 1 from the grid (Ø 15.22, RMS 3.3, incl. wells clipped at the mosaic edge); `plate-24_Rossette A1` (low-contrast fluorescence) 23/24, **Ø 15.59** — the case the ±30 % band got wrong at 18.38 mm, the one that motivated the tighter default. Robustness: 4 wells blacked out → 20 measured + 4 from the grid, rest ≤ 8.8 px; illumination gradient 0.55→1.45× → ≤ 0.2 px; noise σ25 → ≤ 0.9 px; rotated 3°/12° → 24/24; ±5 % scale error degrades gracefully + warns. Also **brightness = max over channels, not `BGR2GRAY`** (a fluorescence mosaic can be near-pure blue, which the luma weights all but discard — std 47.8 → 5.6 on the real blue mosaic). **Deliberately NOT done:** the legacy fallback is REMOVED from this button (keeping it would let a wrong grid be placed silently, defeating the gates, and it returns nothing on this plate anyway); the mosaic-scan path keeps its own old detection (not broken, and the finished scan now auto-opens this dialog) — a clean follow-up. **One TEST had to be corrected, not the code:** `test_auto_detect_on_open_places_all_wells` built a synthetic image with 40 px wells 120 px apart while telling the dialog it was a 24-well plate at a scale making those wells 390 px across and 965 px apart — the old detector never read the plate definition so the contradiction didn't matter; a model-driven detector correctly refuses it. **Needs GUI verification on the rig** (Map wells → 24 rings on the wells with the measured Ø in the status line → confirm all → a couple of named wells drive to the right place). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_well_detection.py` (**36** — synthetic plate with a 1.00–1.19 R rim matching the measured real profile, plus a real-mosaic class that skips when the scans aren't on disk); regression plate-types/well-type-presets/mosaic-orientation-remap+adjust/memory-perf/unified-cal/rosette-reanchor/single-well-reregister/startup-well-map/fluor-shift/click-rim/workflow-toggle/fluorescence/v731-integration/last-known-cal/plate-centering/freeform-warp **440 green**. One pre-existing failure confirmed NOT from this change by running the **committed HEAD** copy of `detect_filled_wells` against `24.png` (returns 23, test wants ≥24): `test_v75x_plate_mosaic::test_real_24_well_mosaic`, the legacy blob detector this work doesn't touch — the new detector resolves all 24 on that same image. |
| `MEBP_v75x_MOSAIC_UNREACHABLE_TRAVEL_STALL.md` | v7.5.x **fix — "the full plate mosaic is not able to get past a single row of images"** (operator, 2026-07-30; *"I think there is an issue with the data flow and management"*). Root-caused from the operator's own `logs/app.log`: the whole-plate scan region IS the configured XY safety envelope (`_ploc_whole_plate_scan_bounds → env`; `ME3B V3.json` records `xy_max_x = 116327 µm`) but the stage physically stopped at **X = 80593 µm** and never moved in X again — while **Y kept tracking its commands exactly**, which is what proves real travel exhaustion rather than the known stale-`R`-ack read desync (a desynced read mis-reports BOTH axes). From that column on `_MosaicScanWorker` (a) **ignored `wait_for_xy_arrival`'s return value entirely**, (b) burned the **flat 10 s** timeout on every remaining point, (c) grabbed a frame anyway and **stitched a DUPLICATE tile at the same canvas spot** (feeding the overlap registration a bogus measurement), and (d) hit the serpentine reversal, which starts the next row at the *far, unreachable* end — so row 1 re-ground the same dead band. ~2 min of dead waiting per row against ~30 s of real scanning ⇒ the scan never visibly left row 0, and nothing warned that the mosaic would be silently partial. **Fix — judge arrival from the MEASURED position, never the boolean** (`wait_for_xy_arrival` is documented to time out spuriously under the stale-`R` desync, so gating on it would DROP tiles the stage did reach; a garbled `(None,None,None)` read degrades to the legacy place-at-target behaviour), **don't stitch an off-target tile** (`_ARRIVE_TOL_UM`=150 µm — its frame shows somewhere else), and reuse that ONE read for placement (the tile used to read position twice, once in the wait + once after the grab). **Distance-sized arrival timeout** `2·dist/max_xy_speed + 2 s` clamped 3–20 s (a dead 3.1 mm step costs ~3 s, not 10). **Runtime reachable-travel box** (`_note_unreachable`/`_reachable`) clipped only after **3 consecutive** non-arrivals — three, so one comms hiccup can't permanently truncate a 900-tile scan — and only on the axis AND direction that fell short (a stage short in +X keeps its whole Y range); points outside it are then **skipped before any move** while still emitting `progress` so `n/total` reaches the end. Worker exposes `unreachable_skipped`/`reach_note`; `_ploc_on_mosaic_finished` captures them before releasing the worker and warns that the mosaic is partial. **Skip-and-continue, NOT abort** — the reachable area still maps correctly, so aborting would discard good tiles. **The envelope-vs-origin question is deliberately left to the operator:** the taught map spans X 10162→106427 µm with `taught_a1=(105618, 65890)`, i.e. the stage COULD reach ~106 mm when taught, and a Prior sets position 0 at power-on wherever the stage sits — so a saved *absolute* envelope + taught well map only line up across a power cycle if the stage starts from the same place; both causes are named in the message rather than guessed, since silently rewriting `safety_limits` would guess at a value governing every clamped move on the machine. **Also fixed en route:** the **`comtypes` DEBUG firehose** — the Nikon Ti panel polls the COM object model ~1 Hz and `comtypes` logs ~10 DEBUG lines per COM call, ~**35 000 lines / several MB during this one 4-minute scan**, rotating `logs/app.log` every few minutes and burying the app's own output (diagnosing this required filtering it out); `main.py::setup_logging` now pins `comtypes` (+`PIL`/`matplotlib`) to WARNING while the app's own modules stay DEBUG. Deliberately NOT changed: this run's **5 % overlap / 2 ms settle** — the code already warns about both at scan start (25 % / 300 ms recommended) and they're operator knobs. **Needs real-HW verification** (scan advances past row 1 at ~1 s/tile; if travel is still short the dialog names the stopping point; then settle whether `xy_max_x` is too large or the origin moved — drive to a known taught well and see if the needle lands on it). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_mosaic_unreachable_travel.py` (9 — completes-not-hangs, 12/39 tiles stitched with **15 moves instead of 39**, short-axis-only clipping, single-transient-miss doesn't clip, arrived-tile-stitched-despite-lying-wait, failed-read fallback, timeout scaling/clamp/no-`safety_limits`); regression plate-mosaic worker/UI/bounds/settings/detect-fit/reanchor/overlay/reentrancy + orientation-remap/orientation-adjust/memory-perf/unified-cal/rosette-reanchor/single-well-reregister/startup-well-map/fluor-shift/click-rim/workflow-toggle/fluorescence/nikon-ti/z-retract **309 green** (`TestManualAlignPage` excluded — documented pre-existing hang). |
| `MEBP_v75x_MOSAIC_MEMORY_AND_OVERLAY_PERF.md` | v7.5.x **fix — mosaic capture crawls past ~300 tiles (RAM 80-90%) + app laggy after build** (`changes_needed.md` items 1 & 2). **Root cause:** `MosaicBuilder._records` retained EVERY raw frame (~2 MB → ~½ GB at 300 tiles) + the float64 `_composite`/`_weight_sum` (~190 MB) lived the whole scan → swap thrash → seconds/tile; and `JogWorkspaceView._paint_mosaic_overlay` re-scaled the ~18 MB mosaic pixmap with a 180° painter transform on EVERY paintEvent. **Fix:** new `MosaicBuilder(retain_frames=False)` frees each tile's frame right after `stitch_incremental` blends it (full-plate scan only — that path never re-blends; per-well `build_mosaic` + the calibration dialog `tile_images_px` keep `retain_frames=True`; `build_mosaic`/`_ensure_composite`/`tile_images_px` skip freed frames defensively); new `free_accumulators()` releases the float64 buffers post-scan keeping the uint8 display cache (called by `_MosaicScanWorker.run` after detection); the overlay now caches the scaled+rotated pixmap keyed by `(w,h,flip,source-id)` (pan/needle-motion reuse it; the manual nudge is size-invariant; invalidated on `set_mosaic_overlay`). A per-tile preview-emit throttle was prototyped + reverted (broke the one-preview-per-tile worker contract; the memory fixes alone resolve the slowdown). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_mosaic_memory_and_overlay_perf.py` (8); plate-mosaic + orientation-remap green (only the documented pre-existing CV `test_real_24_well_mosaic` fails). |

| `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_TRAVEL_SPLIT.md` | v7.5.x **fix — Quick Print pump doesn't stop + Z doesn't retract across "quick moves to a new location"** (`changes_needed.md` item 5). **Root cause:** `_obj_dict_to_path_points` flattened each object to XY only (dropping the Z-lift/pump travel columns) and `_path_segments_for_selection` split per OBJECT only, so a pen-up travel inside an object (multi-shape sketch / CSV) printed at fixed Z. **Fix (operator chose split + relief):** new `_obj_dict_to_trajectory` + `_travel_mask` (pump-not-advancing primary, top-Z-band fallback, `None` for flat→legacy single segment) + `_obj_dict_to_subpaths` split each object into print sub-paths at travel; `build_well_plate_job`'s existing seam machinery (lift→hop→lower→prime, PRINT_PATH-only extrusion) then stops the pump + retracts Z between them. The "relief" half needed **no new code** — the existing `PrintManager._print_pump_suckback("quick_move")` (user WIP, gated by `pump_relief_on_quick_move`) already fires on each hop the split creates, re-primed by the next segment's prime (an initial attempt to ALSO emit suck-back/re-prime DISPENSE commands was reverted — it duplicated that → double suck-back). Only `quick_print_workflow.py` changed. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_quick_print_travel_split.py` (7); seam + quick-print pick&place + pump-relief green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_NEEDLE_MAX_FLOW_AND_PICKUP_RATES.md` | v7.5.x **needle-derived max pump flow — display + flow-bounded print speed** (`changes_needed.md` item 6: too-high flow ingests air; show the calc; bound print speed; user-settable pickup/deposit rates). The backend ceiling (`SafetyLimits.update_from_hardware_config`→`FlowPhysics.max_safe_flow_rate_uL_s`, water-ref viscosity → hard clamp in `move_pump_uL`), the `StageController` speed resolvers, and per-workflow pickup/deposit rate spinboxes already existed (user WIP). Added: **(a)** Hardware Setup → Pump readout `_pump_maxflow_lbl` + `_refresh_max_flow_display()` (recomputes from the needle; refreshed on config change/load); **(b)** Quick Print `_max_pump_flow_uL_s()` (MagicMock-`__float__`-guarded) + `_flow_limited_xy_max_mm_s()` = `min(xy_max, max_flow/(bore_area×modifier))`, used as the 100% anchor for BOTH speed and flow so flow@100% never exceeds the ceiling and the bead width stays correct (speed reduced instead of flow silently clamped). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_needle_max_flow_and_pickup_rates.py` (6); quick-print/seam/pump suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_RELIEF_AND_BORE_BEAD_MODEL.md` | v7.5.x **two operator-confirmed changes from the workflow-move audit (`coding plans/Analyses/MEBP_WORKFLOW_MOVE_AUDIT.md`): F-1 one bead model + F-4 redesigned pressure relief.** **F-1 (bead model):** the deposited volume-per-mm is now **inner-bore cross-section × extrusion modifier** everywhere (the "thickness of the laid line"), replacing the legacy **outer-Ø × layer_height** in `FlowPhysics.extrusion_flow_rate` + `GeometryEngine.compute_pump_positions`/`compute_total_volume` (all gain a `modifier=1.0` kwarg; `layer_height` kept as a param but **no longer scales volume** — it is now only the Z step between stacked layers). Quick Print already used this model; this aligns the trajectory / Print-Builder path. `PrintObject.extrusion_modifier` field added (default 1.0, serialized) so the data model is ready — **per-object modifier UI in Print Builder is a tracked follow-up** (the trajectory path runs at modifier 1.0, byte-stable canonical bore model). `line_spacing` (fill raster pitch) deliberately left on `od_mm` (separate fill-density lever). **F-4 (pressure relief):** relief is now **% of each pump's syringe volume** (`HardwareConfig.pump_relief_percent`, default **1 %**, tunable ~0.001–5 %; per-pump µL = pct/100 × syringe vol) — replaces the old absolute `pump_relief_volume_uL` (legacy value discarded on load → default 1 %). Three independent **toggles, all default ON** (`pump_relief_on_pickup/deposit/quick_move`). `StageController` gains `pump_relief_percent()`/`pump_relief_uL(pump)`/`pump_relief_on_*()`; `move_pump_uL(relieve=True)` is now **direction-aware**: an ASPIRATE (pickup) → DISPENSE the relief back (bleed vacuum, existing); a DISPENSE (deposit) → ASPIRATE the relief back (**suck-back, stop drool**, new), each gated by its toggle. `PrintManager._print_pump_suckback` does the **deposit** suck-back (end of each `_execute_print_path`) and the **quick-move** suck-back (before each inter-object hop `MOVE_XY`; re-primed by the next object's prime) — the quick-move case also addresses `changes_needed.md` item 5. **Scope decision:** deposit/quick-move relief is **ink-printing only** — the volume-balanced spheroid/cell **micro-captures keep `relieve=False`** (a 1 %-of-syringe relief = ~2.5 µL would dwarf their nL volumes and break net-zero). **GUI:** Hardware Setup → Pump "Pump Timing" relief spin → **% of syringe** + 3 checkboxes (`_rebuild_config`/`_apply_config_to_ui`); the 4 prep-workflow "Common — Pump (global)" rows + the Common Print Settings page row swapped to `pump_relief_percent`; `CommonPrintSettings.GLOBAL_KEYS`/`DEFAULTS` updated. **F-2 follow-on:** the global 10 s settle was a residual-pressure workaround now superseded by this relief — operator can lower `pump_settle_time_s` (config, no code). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1/V3.** Tests: new `tests/test_v75x_pump_relief_and_bead_model.py` (14: bore-area math, modifier scaling, layer-height-ignored, %→µL per pump, deposit/quick-move suck-back + toggle gating, migration); updated `test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (relief now % + direction-aware) + `test_v75x_common_print_settings.py` (key rename); relief/settings/bead (71) + workflow-settings/4-workflow-pages/sketch (153) + print-path (62) + config-serialize (85) suites green. Pre-existing failures (confirmed by stashing): `test_v73_trajectory_planner.TestFullPlanGeneration` (6) + `test_v75x_pump_plunger_setup.TestDirectionOwnershipInMotion` (3, an uncommitted `MotionEstimator` change makes `move_pump_relative` read `_pos_poller` a `__new__`-partial test controller lacks). |

| `MEBP_v75x_JOG_PUMP_FILL_READOUT.md` | v7.5.x **fix — the Jog page syringe showed no current volume and never auto-updated** (operator). **Root cause:** the Jog pump rack (`gui/widgets/pump_rack.py` → `PumpColumn._refresh`) rendered `fc.ink_volume_uL` from the **static** `pump_cfg.fluid_column` — a layered oil/buffer/ink model only mutated by `aspirate_ink`/`dispense` **during a print/workflow**, never during manual jogging — so it displayed a frozen config value (usually `0.0 µL`). The ~300 ms status tick (`app.py::_tick`→`_update_status`→`page.on_status_update`→`_refresh_pump_panel`) *was* firing; it just refreshed a constant. Both halves of the complaint had this one cause. The Hardware **Control Panel** was already reframed in v7.5.x (`MEBP_v75x_PUMP_PLUNGER_CAL_AND_ASPIRATE_DISPENSE.md`) to show live fill via `controller.raw_to_pump_fill_uL`; the Jog rack was never migrated. **Fix (mirror the control panel):** new `PumpColumn.set_live_fill(*, fill_uL, capacity_uL, raw_mm, syringe, ink_spec, calibrated)` + `PumpRack.update_live_fills(fills)`; `JogControlPage._refresh_pump_panel` rewritten to derive each tick from `is_pump_plunger_calibrated`/`raw_to_pump_fill_uL`(RAW Marlin mm→µL)/`pump_capacity_uL` and feed the rack. **Calibrated** → fill µL (0=empty→capacity=full) + proportional bar + low-fill warning; **configured but un-calibrated** → live raw Marlin mm + "calibrate plunger for µL" hint; **no syringe** → "not configured". The barrel now renders a single fill level (no live oil/buffer/ink layering on the Jog page). `-0.0` at the empty datum normalised to `0.0` (genuine negatives kept). **Adversarial review (3 lenses→verify):** fixed (a) un-calibrated mm frame divergence — show raw `p_raw` to match the embedded control panel; (b) ink accent read the non-existent `InkSpec.display_color` (repo-wide misnomer) → use `.color`. Old `set_pump_loadout`/`update_from_workspace` path left as orphaned back-compat. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (jog a *calibrated* pump → µL readout + bar track the plunger live; un-calibrated → live raw mm + hint). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_jog_pump_fill_readout.py` (12); pump-plunger/jog-nav/axis-map/jog-direction/pump-settle suites green (123). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_TYPES_Z_OFFSETS_AND_MOSAIC.md` | v7.5.x **selectable plate TYPES on Hardware Setup → Plate: per-type Z offsets + auto-loading mosaics** (operator request). A well-count ships as different products (Corning glass-bottom, NEST plastic-bottom, generic) that share the XY grid but differ in **bottom material → different needle Z references**, and each has its **own full-plate mosaic**. **(1)** the automatic Z-needle offsets — **Plate-Bottom, Fast-Move/Safe, Max, AND Plate-Top Z** — now **inherit from the selected plate type** instead of the single global Device offset set (Replace stays manual); **(2)** each type's mosaic/template/well-training auto-loads on select. **Key lever:** `MosaicStore`/`PlateTemplateStore`/`WellTrainingStore` are all keyed by `active_plate_key`, and the reload triggers already fire on plate change — so a **distinct `active_plate_key` per type** makes the data auto-segregate through existing paths. A plate type is a **thin overlay on a base standard format**: geometry inherited (runtime `WellPlate.format` stays the base `int` → same-format guards/`PLATE_DEFINITIONS` unaffected), identity = the type id (`active_plate_key`), Z offsets `{top,bottom,safe,max}` mm below the needle-cam fiducial. **New `SupportClasses/PlateTypeStore.py`** (pure-JSON singleton, mirror of `MosaicStore`): `PlateType` dataclass; built-ins `config/hardware/plate_types/builtin/*.json` + user overrides `…/user/*.json` where **a user entry shadows the built-in by id** (the learn-loop writes a user override; built-in stays pristine). `HardwareConfig.plate_type_id` + `active_plate_key` precedence **type → name → format** (+ serialize + `validate()` dangling-id check). `WellPlate.load` resolves a type id → `from_format(base_format)` (+ optional `well_depth_mm` override; unknown id safe). `StageController` Z offsets gain **`max`** (`set_plate_z_offsets`/`estimate_plate_z_refs`→`plate_max_z`/`apply_z_convention`) + new `_apply_active_plate_type_offsets` pushes the active type's offsets in `set_hardware_config` (generic → keep device-profile fallback; Max Z still **never** written to `safety_limits.z_max`). Calibration: `_zoff_estimate_from_needle_cam` also fills Max Z; new `_apply_plate_type_z_estimates(force=False)` (taught-wins: fills only `None` refs, hooked at the end of `set_hardware_config` after `_load_calibration` so taught values survive boot ordering); **learn-loop button** `_zoff_save_offsets_to_plate_type` (offset = fiducial − user_z(ref) → user PlateType override). Mosaic/template/training keys now derive from `active_plate_key` (`_jog_plate_key`/`_ploc_plate_key`/sub-well key — fixes the base-int collision). **Two-step Format→Type card** above the Plate designer (`hardware_setup.py`): Format combo filters a Plate-type combo (Generic + products) + offsets readout; sets `plate_type_id`, drives the designer's base geometry, syncs on `set_hardware_config`; designer's own picker clears the type. Seeded built-ins: **6/24/96-well × {glass, plastic}** (z_offsets best-guess until taught). **Per-plate-key calibration:** the WHOLE taught calibration (XY taught wells / warp / affine / Z references / Z-plane / reference markers) is **archived per `active_plate_key`** (new `settings.calibration_by_plate`) and **auto-restored when a plate is selected**, so calibration *belongs to the plate* (each type keeps its own map). Dual-section design keeps the flat `settings.calibration` mirroring the ACTIVE plate (every legacy flat reader — `print_setup_legacy`/`app.py` snapshot restore — untouched); new `CalibrationPage` helpers `_calibration_key`/`_calibration_archive` (one-time migrates a legacy flat block under its `plate_format`)/`_reset_calibration_state`/`_activate_calibration_for_key`, `_save_calibration(key=)` dual-writes; a plate switch (`set_hardware_config` key change + in-page `_on_plate_changed`) flushes the OUTGOING plate's block (before the geometry rebuild) then restores the INCOMING plate's (or resets to blank → type-offset auto-fill takes over). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (select Corning-24 → mosaic + Z guesses load; teach floor + Save offsets; teach plate A → switch to B → A's map leaves view → back to A → A's taught wells + Z return; switch to NEST-24 → different mosaic + guesses, shared geometry). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_types.py` (32, +`TestPerPlateCalibration`); plate-mosaic (keys)/mosaic-orientation-remap/cal-z-envelope/last-known-calibration/plate-centering/orientation/printing-mode-calibrated/calibration-revision/plate-location-click-rim/quick-print/ink-well-type/z-axis/workflow-settings/camera-cal-store/pump suites green (1 pre-existing unrelated CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_COMMON_PRINT_SETTINGS_PAGE.md` | v7.5.x new **Common Print Settings** page (Workflows mode, ⚙ tile) collecting the settings common to ALL workflows (operator request). **Two classes, one mutation bus:** (1) **Global params** — `pump_settle_time_s` (dwell), `pump_relief_volume_uL` (pressure relief), `pump_prime_time_s` (prime) — canonically on `HardwareConfig` (single source of truth the controller reads); one value, no override. (2) **Promoted prep defaults** — `service_z`/`prep_rate`/`oil_needles`/`buffer_needles`/`wash_cycles`/`wash_z_amp`/`wash_xy_amp`/`wash_dwell` — shared DEFAULTS each prep workflow **INHERITS** but can **OVERRIDE** locally (operator's chosen model). New pure-Python `SupportClasses/CommonPrintSettings.py` (proxies globals from a live HardwareConfig ref; holds promoted defaults dict persisted in settings.json `common_print_settings`; `get`/`set`-then-notify, `add_listener`, coercion). `WorkflowSettingsDialog` extended: `SettingsSection.add_common(key,label,widget,default,common_key=,overridable=)` — overridable adds an "Override" checkbox (off = disabled widget force-set to the common value, so the workflow's existing `widget.value()` runtime read returns the effective value unchanged; on = local value); non-overridable (globals) writes straight through via `common.set`. `dialog.set_common(model)` re-syncs links; `apply()` runs a **migration** (legacy saved value with no `__ovr` flag → override iff it differs from the common default, so existing customisations are preserved). New `gui/pages/workflows/common_print_settings_workflow.py` page (Pump-global + Needle-prep-defaults + read-only calibration heights) edits the model directly; tile in `workflow_picker.py`; dispatch + `set_common_print_settings` fan-out in `workflows_mode.py`. `gui/app.py` owns the model (restores promoted from settings.json, refreshes its HardwareConfig ref on every config change, listener persists globals→HW config / promoted→settings.json + re-fans-out, startup fan-out). The 4 prep workflows (spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print) promote their prep fields to `add_common` + add a "Common — Pump (global)" section + `set_common_print_settings`→`dlg.set_common`; fluorescence/stress/timing untouched (no prep). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (edit common dwell/relief/prep default → reflected on Hardware Setup + inherited by un-overridden workflows; per-workflow Override holds a local value; restart preserves). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_common_print_settings.py` (22); workflow-settings/spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print/pump-settle/pump-plunger suites green (230). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_POST_ASPIRATE_PRESSURE_RELIEF.md` | v7.5.x **post-aspirate pump pressure relief — a setup parameter** (operator request). After picking up a reagent (ink/oil/buffer) the residual needle vacuum ingests air during inter-well travel (compliance); dispensing a small, experimentally-derived volume back at the pickup well bleeds it. Mirrors the sibling global pump params: new `HardwareConfig.pump_relief_volume_uL` (µL, default 0 = disabled, non-neg-coerced, serialized) + `StageController.pump_relief_volume_uL()` reader + a `relieve` kwarg on `StageController.move_pump_uL`. When `relieve=True` AND the move is an ASPIRATE (`volume_uL < 0`), after the aspirate+settle completes it dispenses `pump_relief_volume_uL` back via a single recursive `move_pump_uL` (positive → the `<0` guard makes it non-recursive); no-op when volume is 0 / it's a dispense / `relieve=False`. Relief fires **at the pickup well before any travel** (inside the same `move_pump_uL`). `PickAndPlaceManager._settled_pump_move(relieve=)` passthrough (TypeError fallback chain: full→settle-only→plain); `relieve=True` set at the reagent-pickup sites only — `run_prep` oil+buffer aspirates, `run_post_clean` buffer reload, `prepare_starting_oil` oil aspirate, `aspirate_ink` (Quick Print). **Deliberately NOT** applied to volume-balanced captures (spheroid/cell pull, cell-labeling, trypsin/dye) — they must keep exactly what they aspirated. **GUI:** Hardware Setup → Pump "Pump Timing (all pumps)" group gains a "Pressure relief" µL spinbox (wired into `_rebuild_config`/`_apply_config_to_ui`). Net loaded = aspirate − relief (intended; keep relief small). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (set ~0.2–1 µL; confirm small dispense-back per pickup, no air ingestion on travel, print still flows; tune experimentally). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (31, +`TestPumpRelief`); spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print-pick-place (106, unaffected — relief defaults 0) + pump-plunger/workflow-settings offscreen GUI suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PREP_BUFFER_PUMP_DRAIN_BEFORE_TRAVEL.md` | v7.5.x **fix — printing-prep "buffer" step times out and the run aborts before picking up ink** (operator: buffer pickup then "something times out, we never pick up the ink"; recurring). Log: `move_pump_uL(P2, -27.222 µL, 1.000 µL/s)` (4-needle buffer aspirate) → "Prep complete" → `flush_moves: M400 timed out after 15.0s (rx_lines=8, busy=8)` → `safe_travel_to: Z retract M400 timed out — ABORTING`. **Root cause:** buffer prep and the working ink pickup run the **identical** code (`PickAndPlaceManager._settled_pump_move` → `StageController.move_pump_uL(settle=True)`); the only difference is **volume**. A pump `G0` returns on Marlin `ok` = *admitted to the planner*, NOT motion-complete, and the settle-completion wait was `time.sleep(min(move_s, 10s))` — **capped at 10 s**. The 4-needle buffer aspirate (~27 µL @ 1 µL/s ≈ **27 s**) was truncated to 10 s, so `move_pump_uL` returned ~17 s early while Marlin kept running the pump. The next step (`aspirate_ink`→`safe_travel_to`) commanded a Z retract + `M400`; Marlin executes moves **sequentially**, so the retract queued *behind* the still-running pump move and `M400` timed out at 15 s (`busy=8` = board alive, just still draining). Ink pickup "worked" only because its ~6.5 µL fit under the 10 s cap. **Fix:** new `StageController._wait_pump_move_complete(move_s)` drains the in-flight pump move via `M400` (`ZPStage.flush_moves`, same mechanism `safe_travel_to` uses for Z) with a timeout **scaled to the estimate** (`move_s + 5 s`, backstop-capped at 180 s; poller suspended; exception-safe); `move_pump_uL(settle=True)` calls it instead of the 10 s-capped sleep, falling back to an open-loop sleep of the **full** estimate only when no `flush_moves` is available (older controller/fake). Constants: drop `_PUMP_MOVE_WAIT_CAP_S`; add `_PUMP_MOVE_DRAIN_TIMEOUT_CAP_S`/`_PUMP_MOVE_DRAIN_MARGIN_S`. Streamed print path + manual jog (`settle=False`) untouched; all discrete `settle=True` callers (prep/clean/cell/trypsin/dye/print prime) benefit. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (prep: buffer aspirate completes → travel-to-ink no M400 timeout → ink picked up → print starts; `logs/zp_serial.log` M400 after buffer returns `ok`). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (23, +`TestMovePumpSettleM400Confirm`); spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print-pick-place/simple-pm/ZP/stress suites green (pre-existing unrelated `test_v75x_print_setup_routine` prime-amount failures confirmed present with this fix stashed). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINTING_MODE_USES_CALIBRATED_WELLS.md` | v7.5.x **fix — Printing-mode print drove to the stage origin (0,0) at the wrong Z and ignored calibration** (operator: calibrated the plate + assigned inks/washes, hit Run, the needle went to `(0,0)` and never reached the assigned wells). Log proof: `PRINT: well C2 — first point (-22.5, -42.9)` → `XY clamped: (-22518.5,-42936.4) → (0.0,0.0)` → `wait_for_xy_arrival timeout`. **Root cause:** the ENTIRE Printing-mode print path computed every well centre **geometrically** — `WellPlate.get_well_position(name)` returns a PLATE-LOCAL A1-relative offset (A1 at origin) — × `controller.plate_axis_sign()` (`(-1,-1)` on ME3B V1), then fed it to `move_xy_absolute(from_zero_ref=True)` as a zero-ref mm coordinate. With `zero_position` = the needle-zero `(0,0)`, the well grid was anchored at the **stage origin** and the `(-1,-1)` flip made every print/travel well NEGATIVE → clamped to `(0,0)`; service wells (`_find_well`, **unsigned** raw geometric) went to `(0,19300)`/`(19300,0)` — also wrong. The **taught/calibrated** positions (C2 = `(86028,28274)` µm, A1 = `(107561,68633)` µm) live on the Calibration page (`_calibrated_positions`, exposed via `get_calibration_data()`), were wired to **Jog + Workflows** but **never to the Printing-mode setup page** (Quick Print was unaffected — it already resolves calibrated first). So the print ignored calibration entirely; the "wrong Z" was the needle descending at the clamped `(0,0)` corner. **Fix (one resolver, calibrated → zero-ref mm, geometric × sign fallback = byte-identical legacy):** new `PrintSettings.well_positions_mm` (calibrated zero-ref mm by name, stamped at job-build time) + new `PrintTrajectoryPlanner.resolve_well_xy_mm(name, plate, settings)`; `_well_xy` delegates to it (planner stores `self._settings` in `generate()`/`generate_inwell_print()`) → fixes the trajectory + all service wells. `HybridPlanExecutor` (the DEFAULT mode that ran) gains `_well_xy_mm` used in `_execute_print_step` / `TRAVEL_XY` / `_execute_service` (resolves the role-matched well via the resolver, not `_find_well`'s raw offset) / `estimate_time`. `print_setup_legacy` gains `set_calibration_data` + `_well_xy_zref_mm` (discrete `_build_job` resolves calibrated; `_get_settings` stamps `well_positions_mm`). `WizardPrintSetupPage.set_calibration_data` forwards to `_legacy`; `gui/app.py` wires `cal_page.calibration_data_changed → setup_page.set_calibration_data` + pushes once at startup (mirrors the Jog/Workflows wiring). `PrintPlanOfAction._signed_well_xy` also delegates to the resolver (latent fix; only the test-only `plan_to_commands` path uses it). Safety invariants (retract-before-XY, end-at-safe-Z, plate-bottom floor) untouched — only *where* XY travels is corrected. **SECOND bug, same cause — "Z was not moving up between wells":** the detached wizard page also can't reach app settings (`_app_settings` never assigned + `parent=None`), so `travel_z_height` stayed at the **5.0 mm** spinbox default and `print_z_height` at **0.1 mm**. On ME3B V1 (plate bottom 6.06, larger zero-ref Z = higher) **5.0 mm is BELOW the plate**, so "retract to safe Z" drove the needle **DOWN**. Fix sources these from already-wired data: `set_calibration_data`'s `safe_z` (= cal Fast-Move Z) drives `travel_z_spin` (range widened) → both paths retract to the real safe height; `_calibration_plate_bottom_z` prefers `controller.get_plate_bottom_z()` (pushed by `app.py::_update_print_floor_datum`) so `_apply_plate_relative_print_z` derives the right print Z; `_get_settings` sets `top_z_height` from `controller.get_plate_top_z()`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (re-run the Box print → retracts to safe Z + travels to each taught well, service wells correct, prints at the right height, ends at safe Z). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_printing_mode_calibrated_wells.py` (16, incl. offscreen wizard-shell→legacy forward + travel/print-Z-from-calibration smoke); orientation/multi-object/planner-barrier/print-setup-routine/always-safe-z/z-retract/quick-print/trajectory/z-reference suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINT_SETUP_PRINT_MANAGER_FORWARD.md` | v7.5.x **fix — Printing-mode Start was a silent no-op** ("`No print_manager on setup page`" logged once per Start press; print never launched). The v7.5.0 Print Builder refactor made the Printing-mode setup page the `WizardPrintSetupPage` shell, which composes the legacy `PrintSetupPage` as `self._legacy` — and the `PrintManager` is owned by the **legacy** page. `gui/app.py` reaches it as `setup_page.print_manager` from five sites (`_on_monitor_start` Start / `_wire_print_manager_to_monitor` recorder+bridge / Pause / Resume / Abort), but the wizard shell never forwarded the attribute, so `hasattr(setup_page,"print_manager")` was False → `_on_monitor_start` logged + returned and the others `hasattr`-guarded into no-ops. Fix = a read-only `print_manager` **property** on `WizardPrintSetupPage` → `getattr(self._legacy,"print_manager",None)` (returns the live manager so `setup.print_manager.recorder = …` still mutates the real object; fixes all five sites; **Quick Print** was unaffected — it owns its own manager). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (Printing mode: build job → Monitor → Start launches; Pause/Resume/Abort work). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_setup_print_manager_forward.py` (4); quick-print/print-setup-routine/hybrid suites green (22). |
| `MEBP_v75x_INK_WELL_TYPE_AND_SUBTYPE.md` | v7.5.x **split the overloaded `InkSpec.ink_type` into a primary WELL TYPE + an informational INK SUBTYPE, and filter the per-pump ink list to printable inks only** (operator request). **(1) Pump list:** the per-pump "Inks" checklist (Hardware Setup → Pump) was built from `list(ink_library.keys())` — *every* reagent incl. service wells; now filtered to **printable inks only** via new `HardwareSetupPage._pump_ink_names()` (excludes wash/waste/buffer/oil — by well type OR by an ink literally named one of those roles) applied at both build sites (`_refresh_pump_ink_exclusions` + `_apply_config_to_ui`). **(2) Taxonomy:** `ink_type` is now strictly the **well type** ∈ {ink, wash, buffer, waste, oil} (drives ALL behavior — service-well resolution, `well_role_for_ink_type`, colors — **unchanged**); a printable `ink` may also carry an editable, informational `ink_subtype` (presets: granular material / fluorescent stains / cells / hydrogel monomer / media / cell removal reagents / ELISA Beads / Growth Factor Beads). **Data model (`PhysicalModels`):** new `WELL_TYPES`/`SERVICE_WELL_TYPES`/`INK_SUBTYPES` + `is_service_reagent`/`is_printable_ink_type`/`split_legacy_ink_type`; `InkSpec` gains `ink_subtype` (default `ink_type` "custom"→"ink"); **migration in `from_dict` ONLY** (legacy material types granular/cells/hydrogel/media/custom → `ink`+subtype; service types pass through; direct construction NOT migrated so `InkSpec(ink_type="hydrogel")` callers/tests are unaffected). Auto-upgrades on load+save. **Behavioral site:** `FlowPhysics` cell-shear `has_cells` now also honors `ink_subtype=="cells"` (superset of the legacy `ink_type=="cells"`, no regression). **GUI:** `InkEditorDialog` Well-type combo = `WELL_TYPES` + editable Ink-subtype combo (enabled only for `ink`); ink table gains a Subtype column; reagent-location combo/summary, `print_workspace` ink table, and the workflow-settings locations panel show `ink · <subtype>`. Wizard `_load_prefab_inks` → `InkSpec.from_dict` (migrates); `prefab_inks.json` rewritten to the new scheme. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (visual: pump list shows only inks; service-well prep/cleanup still resolves; cell inks still flag shear). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_ink_well_type_and_subtype.py` (30); ink-location/well-colors/quick-print-pick-place/workflow-settings/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/spheroid suites green (172). |
| `MEBP_v75x_FLUORESCENCE_MOSAIC_WORKFLOW.md` | v7.5.x new **Fluorescence Mosaic** workflow (🔬 tile, enabled) — high-resolution multi-channel mosaic of a **single well** (DAPI/FITC/mCherry/Cy5). **No filter-wheel hardware exists** (repo-wide search), so capture is **manual, one full single-well mosaic per channel**: pick objective + well + channels (+ per-channel pseudo-colour), the workflow rasters the whole well per channel and a modal prompts the operator to switch the physical filter between channels. The SAME raster grid + camera scale are reused for every channel → composites register pixel-for-pixel (additive grayscale×colour blend; no inter-channel alignment). **Persistence** = NEW machine-level `SupportClasses/FluorescenceMosaicStore.py` keyed by **(plate, well)** (per-channel PNG under `config/hardware/fluor_mosaics/` + metadata: colour RGB, absolute-µm extent, objective, µm/px, scale; `composite_overlay` per-well blend + `composite_plate_overlay` all-wells-onto-one-canvas; atomic). **Reuse:** `JogWorkspaceView` gains an INDEPENDENT fluorescence overlay layer (`set_fluor_overlay`/`set_fluor_visible`/`has_fluor`/`_paint_fluor_overlay`, separate from the brightfield mosaic slot, drawn under wells, same zero-ref-µm+180°-flip) — inherited by `WorkspaceTargetView`; shared `gui/pages/workflows/_fluorescence_overlay.py` (`load_plate_fluor_overlay`/`load_well_fluor_overlay`/`plate_key_of`/`has_any_fluor`); a "🔬 Fluorescence" toggle wired into Spheroid / Cell Targeting / Cell Labeling / Jog plate view (re-applied on `set_calibration_data`). **Page** `gui/pages/workflows/fluorescence_mosaic_workflow.py` (`FluorescenceMosaicWorkflowPage` + `_SingleWellMosaicWorker` = trimmed copy of `calibration._MosaicScanWorker`, no detection, to avoid importing the calibration page; objective combo applies calibrated µm/px like the HW card; **top row = objective · filter-cube toggle pills (`_ChannelPill`, double-click=colour, ✓=captured) · well**; below = 3-section resizable splitter [`WellPlateNavigator` + live `CameraFeedView`] | **pan/scroll-zoom mosaic** (`_ZoomImageView`); ⚙ scan-knob popout). Tile in `workflow_picker.py`, route in `workflows_mode.py`. **Quick Print overlay deferred** (`PrintTrajectoryMonitorView` is a separate auto-fitting view). **Round-2 fixes (workflow-rootcaused + adversarially verified):** (a) mosaic never displayed/saved — `_start_channel_scan` built a FRESH `MosaicBuilder` but `generate_raster_positions` (the only `_init_composite` caller) ran on a throwaway `tmpl`, so the worker's builder kept `composite/extent=None` → stitch no-op → empty viewer + skipped save; fix = init the canvas on the SAME builder (`generate_raster_positions(self._scan_bounds)`). (b) raster didn't cover the well — `effective_um_per_px` returns UN-rescaled base µm/px when the manager has no resolution stamp (normal startup) → FOV too large → too few tiles; fix = new `_microscope_um_per_px` resolves from the objective store rescaled to the LIVE frame width (mirrors `calibration._ploc_microscope_um_per_px`) + `_refresh_objectives` fires `_on_objective_changed` after un-blocking signals. (c) NEW raster grid preview — shared `_compute_raster_plan(well)` (preview==scan) drawn in the mosaic viewer (`_ZoomImageView.set_grid_preview`) AND on the well selection (`WellPlateNavigator.set_raster_grid(cols,rows)` clipped to the well circle); refreshed on well/objective/settings/showEvent; status shows `cols×rows=N tiles/channel`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_fluorescence_mosaic.py` (25, incl. synchronous worker→composite, grid-coverage, µm/px-rescale); jog-nav/plate-mosaic/quick-print/spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/workflow-settings suites green (1 pre-existing unrelated CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_CELL_LABELING.md` | v7.5.x new **Cell Labeling / staining** workflow (enables the stubbed `cell_labeling` tile) — a sibling of Cell Targeting & Removal with operator-requested differences: deposit **and** aspirate are both **slow**, the headline knob is the **stain incubation time**, and there is **NO placement** — you only select *regions to stain* + a *stain reagent*; after incubation the recovered stain is dumped to **waste**. Per region: load stain → travel to region (off bottom) → SLOW deposit (needle inner area × depth) → incubate (user time) → SLOW aspirate ×multiplier (default 2×) → travel to waste + dispense. Pump volume-balanced per region (load − deposit − aspirate + waste-dispense = 0). Optional needle prep brackets the loop (waste→oil→wash→buffer) + optional clean after (waste→wash→buffer). **Backend (`PickAndPlaceManager`):** new `OperationType.CELL_LABELING` + `CellLabelingConfig` (`compute_deposit_volume_uL`/`compute_aspirate_volume_uL`, slow `deposit_speed_uL_s`/`aspirate_speed_uL_s`, `stain_dwell_time_s`, `aspirate_multiplier`, `label_z_offset_mm`) + dispatch + `_execute_cell_labeling` (reuses `pick_z_mm`=label height, `waste_well_pos`/`service_z_mm` for the always-required dump). **Widget (`live_target_picker`):** new `pick_only` ctor kwarg hides the Place section + Mode toggle, forces pick mode, "N region(s) selected" status (paired behaviour unchanged when omitted). **GUI (`cell_labeling_workflow.py`, NEW):** modelled on the cell-targeting page; pick_only picker; ⚙ Settings popout (Stain incubation = headline dwell / Region height / Pump & stain reagent / Stain deposit-removal / Needle prep-clean / Motion); Start gate (≥1 region + bore + Safe Z + ZP connected + calibrated plate-bottom + stain reagent assigned/calibrated + **waste well** assigned/calibrated — the dump is core, so `service_z` stays enabled even with prep/clean off). Cross-position moves route through `safe_travel_to`; mid-run ZP drop → `AbortException`; ends at Safe Z. Wired via `workflow_picker.py` (tile enabled) + `workflows_mode.py`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_cell_labeling.py` (23); cell-targeting/spheroid/quick-print/picker-scaling/workflow-settings suites green (149). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_PLUNGER_CAL_AND_ASPIRATE_DISPENSE.md` | v7.5.x **per-pump plunger zero/max calibration (mirrors the Z stage) + ubiquitous ASPIRATE/DISPENSE vocabulary** (operator request). **Calibration:** new `StageController.apply_pump_setup(pump, raw_dispensed, raw_aspirated)` — the pump twin of `apply_z_setup`. ZERO = plunger all the way IN (syringe empty → fill 0); MAX = all the way OUT (full). One capture flow sets the datum (`zero_position[pump]`), **DERIVES** the dispense/aspirate direction, and sets the soft-limit envelope (`safety_limits.p{n}_min/max`, exact captured extremes — no margin, so the plunger is never commanded past its hard stops). Helpers: `pump_aspirate_sign`/`set_pump_aspirate_sign`, `is_pump_plunger_calibrated`, `pump_dir_sign` (= `−aspirate_sign` once calibrated, else legacy `_flip_sign`), `capture_current_pump_raw`, `apply_pump_convention` (startup restore), fill readout `pump_capacity_uL`/`raw_to_pump_fill_uL`/`pump_fill_uL`/`get_pump_setup`. **Direction OWNERSHIP (operator chose "just like Z"):** `move_pump_relative` applies `pump_dir_sign` (was `_flip_sign`); the Xbox jog (`ZPJogHandler`) gains a `pump_dir_sign_provider` and uses it for pump deltas — so a calibrated pump's direction comes from the calibration, retiring the per-pump `steps_per_mm`-sign/`axis_flip` hack. Uncalibrated pumps keep the legacy path (no change until calibrated). **Persistence:** `DeviceProfile.pump_setup` (×5 methods) + `main.py` `apply_pump_convention` restore; `SafetyLimits.update_from_hardware_config(skip_pumps=)` clobber-guard so the calibrated envelope wins over the syringe-stroke estimate. **GUI:** Hardware Setup → Pump "Pump Plunger Setup" group (pump selector + Set Dispensed/Set Aspirated + confirm dialog + capacity readout, mirrors stage_panel's Z setup); control_panel P1/P2/P3 readout reframed to **fill µL** (0=empty→capacity=full, bar 0→capacity), raw mm in the tooltip, falls back to raw mm when uncalibrated. **Vocabulary (aspirate & dispense ubiquitous; NOT "inject"):** canonical block atop `move_pump_uL` + CLAUDE.md principle #4. `CommandType.EXTRUDE`→`DISPENSE` (member renamed, **value kept `"extrude"`** so exec-log manifests stay comparable); `PickPlaceExecutor.post_expel_needles`→`post_dispense_needles` + workflow widget key/label; `StageController.extrude_uL`→`dispense_uL` (+ thin alias); `WellSetup.WasteBehavior/SortedCellBehavior` `eject_rate_uL_s`→`dispense_rate_uL_s` with a translating `from_dict` shim (old saved JSON still loads); stress-test `do_extrude`/`_chk_extrude`/`_extrude_burst`→`dispense`; scattered expel/eject/draw strings+comments → dispense/aspirate. Carve-outs left as-is: print-physics `extrusion` (`FlowPhysics`/`GeometryEngine`), Marlin `M302`/`M83`, `prime`, `retract`, cell-targeting `push`/`pull`, spheroid `pickup`/`release`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (per pump: jog all-in→Set Dispensed reads fill 0; all-out→Set Aspirated reads ≈capacity + direction_ok; confirm aspirate raises fill / dispense lowers it; soft limits stop over-aspirate/over-dispense; restart restores). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_plunger_setup.py` (19, incl. offscreen GUI smoke); pump/print/cell-targeting/spheroid/quick-print/stress/workflow-settings suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_ORIENTATION_CONVENTION.md` | v7.5.x **ONE canonical well-plate orientation everywhere: well A1 displays TOP-LEFT; stage physical 0,0 is BOTTOM-RIGHT** (ME3B V1 Prior origin bottom-right, +X/+Y toward top-left). Fixes three operator symptoms — screens disagreed visually (the 180° flip was a constant duplicated in 2 views, absent elsewhere), motion drove to the MIRRORED well (geometric well→stage prediction assumed plate axes ALIGN with stage axes, but on ME3B they're anti-aligned), and there was no documented standard. **Single source of truth:** new per-machine `plate_flip_180` (default True) — `DeviceProfile.plate_flip_180` + `config/hardware/devices/ME3B V1.json` + `StageController.plate_flip_180()`/`plate_axis_sign()` (`(-1,-1)` when flipped) + `set_plate_flip_180`, restored at startup via `apply_z_convention(plate_flip_180=)` (mirrors the `z_up_sign` plumbing; `getattr`-guarded for `__new__` stubs); a Hardware Setup → Device → Z-Axis-Setup checkbox persists it via `_persist_active_profile`. **Display (drives the flip from the setting, keeps the reflect-about-envelope-centre involution so clicks round-trip):** `JogWorkspaceView` + `PrintTrajectoryMonitorView` `_FLIP_DISPLAY_180` constant → instance `_flip_180` + `set_plate_flip_180`, pushed from `jog_control`/`quick_print_workflow`; **`print_monitor.PlateOverviewWidget`** hybrid fix (wells plate-local but the live needle was fed zero-ref stage-frame → mirrored) negates the needle when flipped. **Geometry (the data model `WellPlate` is plate-local and UNCHANGED):** `get_all_positions_from_a1`/`get_a1_from_plate_center`/`get_all_positions_from_plate_center`/`get_well_area_bounds_*` gain an optional `plate_axis_sign=(1,1)` (default = byte-identical legacy; bounds re-normalise min/max under a negative sign); all `calibration.py` geometric prediction/seed/fallback sites + `jog_control` startup seed + `SimulatedCamera._draw_wells` pass `controller.plate_axis_sign()`. **Warp interplay:** the sign is applied ONLY to the prediction; `PlateWarpCalibrator` is untouched, so a fresh teach re-solves on sign-corrected predictions and taught wells reproduce measured exactly (no double-apply). **Persistence guard (safety):** `_save_calibration` stamps `plate_flip_180`; `_load_calibration` discards the stale taught XY calibration (taught_a1/corner/warp/markers) + forces a re-teach when the stamp ≠ current (else the warp's predicted-leg drives to garbage); `CalibrationSnapshotStore` fingerprint carries orientation. **Print-execution motion (needle-safety-critical):** every GEOMETRIC `get_well_position`-based well centre is sign-mapped — `PrintManager.HybridPlanExecutor._plate_axis_sign`, `print_setup_legacy` well_positions, `PrintTrajectoryPlanner._well_xy` (incl. service wells) + `PrintPlanOfAction._signed_well_xy` via new `PrintSettings.plate_axis_sign` (stamped in the GUI builders), Quick Print's geometric fallback; calibrated/taught well centres are left as-is. Malformed sign degrades to aligned `(1,1)` (never crashes a print). `WellSetup` Z-plane fit left untouched (self-consistent plate-local frame). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (re-teach a full plate, "Go To" named wells + run a small multi-well print → correct physical wells, ends at safe Z; loading a pre-orientation cal prompts a re-teach). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_orientation_convention.py` (17); plate-centering/freeform-warp/predict-well/last-known-cal/jog-nav/sim-camera/trajectory-view/z-axis/plate-design/rosette suites green (188). **Addendum — mosaic remap (existing cal was stale after the flip → navigated to the mirror well; a bogus warp had absorbed the 180° as a ~79 mm correction):** the mosaic is ground truth (captured at trusted absolute stage positions), so wells are RE-DERIVED from the mosaic's detected centres and RELABELLED for the current orientation, stored DIRECTLY (no warp). New `SupportClasses/MosaicWellRemap.py` (`label_positions` = orientation-aware relabel of fixed centres — A1 = max-stageX/maxY corner under `(-1,-1)`; `detect_raw_positions`/`detect_well_positions` = detect saved mosaic + back-project). New explicit `calibrated_positions` persistence in `calibration.py` (`_save_calibration` writes it when set with no warp; `_load_calibration` restores it DIRECTLY and SUPPRESSES warp/affine reconstruction, added to the orientation-guard pop-list; `_has_plate_calibration` recognises it). New Plate-Location button **"Re-derive wells from saved mosaic (ground truth)"** → `_ploc_rederive_from_saved_mosaic` (clears stale taught/warp/affine, relabels the most-complete ground-truth source, stores directly, NO motion). One-shot `tools_regen_plate_orientation.py` already regenerated the 24-well `settings.json` + `last_calibration.json` (new A1 = (104915,68044), dropped the bogus warp/affine; `*.bak-orientremap` backups). **Follow-ups:** (a) `_CalibrationPlateView` showed "top-left well = D6" because its predicted/calibrated dots were positioned relative to a desync-able `_taught_a1` (with a latent µm/mm mix) — now drawn **by NAME on each well's plate-local grid cell** (A1 always top-left, robust to `taught_a1` desync); the regen also resyncs `taught_a1 == calibrated A1`. (b) Whole-plate **mosaic scan now defaults to the FULL reachable XY envelope** (new `_ploc_whole_plate_scan_bounds`) so the entire XY space is mapped (was the well-centre extent); rosette single-well override unchanged; the tile-count confirm dialog surfaces the size. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_mosaic_orientation_remap.py` (14). |
| `MEBP_v75x_INK_LOCATION_WELL_COLORS.md` | v7.5.x **Hardware Setup → Ink → Reagent Locations well colors** (operator request + bug). **Bug fixed:** the assigned well color vanished on mouse-leave and only reappeared on a tab switch — root cause was a **duplicate `WellPlateView.update_all_wells`** (the shadowing v7.3.1-rolecolors copy) that set the brush DIRECTLY via `item.setBrush(...)` and left `WellGraphicsItem._color`/`_role` stale, so `hoverLeaveEvent → _apply_style()` repainted from the stale `EMPTY_WELL_COLOR`; `_refresh_reagent_locations()` on page re-show is why colors "came back". New `PhysicalModels.INK_TYPE_COLORS` + `ink_type_border_color(ink_type)` (oil gets a dedicated peach since `well_role_for_ink_type` maps it to INK; service types reuse role colors; material types fall back to INK blue). `WellGraphicsItem` gains a persisted `_border_color` + `set_appearance(fill, border, label)` (both colors stored so they survive hover); `_apply_style` honors the stored border (`WELL_TYPE_BORDER_WIDTH=2.5`; selection mauve still wins). New `WellPlateView.update_reagent_appearances({well:(fill,border,label)})`; `hardware_setup._refresh_loc_colors` rewritten to feed ink color (fill) + type color (border). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (visual). `_size_spin.isVisibleTo` test preserved). **Stress Test** + **Timing Calibration** popouts host their full config grids as saveable presets. `_build_settings`/`_preflow_s`/`_prime_default_s` made `getattr`-safe for the `__new__`-partial test pages. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_workflow_settings_popout.py` (30: store round-trip, dialog registry/pending-combo, locations panel, spheroid dwell, all 5 pages build + profile round-trip); workflow + print/safe-travel/jog suites green (284). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_SETTLE_AND_PRIME_TIME.md` | v7.5.x **global pump settle time + prime time, configured on Hardware Setup → Pump** (operator request). Two new `HardwareConfig` fields (`pump_settle_time_s`=0.0, `pump_prime_time_s`=0.25; serialized, non-neg-coerced). **Settle** = a blocking dwell applied **before AND after** every *discrete* pump actuation so the fluid/pressure settles and the workflow doesn't advance to the next step until the pump finishes. Centralized at the single µL chokepoint `StageController.move_pump_uL(..., *, settle=False)`: when `settle=True` it sleeps the settle time pre-move, issues the move, **blocks** for the (open-loop) completion (`abs(vol)/rate+0.1`, capped 10 s — byte-identical to the legacy `EXTRUDE` wait it subsumes), then sleeps the settle time post-move; `settle=0` still waits for completion. New `StageController.pump_settle_time_s()` reader. **Discrete callers opt in** (`settle=True`): `PrintManager` `EXTRUDE` handler (subsumes its manual wait; `TypeError` fallback for fakes), the hybrid/service `move_pump` (brackets explicitly to keep its abort-aware wait), and `PickAndPlaceManager` via new module helper `_settled_pump_move(ctrl, …)` at **all 24** spheroid/cell-removal/prep/`aspirate_ink`/trypsin/dye sites (`TypeError` fallback). **Scope decisions (operator-confirmed):** discrete actuations only — **NOT** the streamed per-segment print path (`_execute_print_path` untouched — per-segment settle would dwell→over-extrusion blobs) and **NOT** manual jog (`move_pump_relative` — must stay continuous); one **global** value (not per-pump); single value applied **both** before+after. **Prime** = pre-flow lead-in duration printing workflows use (prime volume = flow × time). Quick Print's per-run "Pre-flow lead-in" Advanced knob (`_preflow`/`_preflow_s`) now **seeds its default from `pump_prime_time_s`** via new `_prime_default_s()` (so the hardware-page value ports in; per-run override preserved). **GUI:** "Pump Timing (all pumps)" group on the Hardware Setup → Pump sub-page (Settle/Prime spinboxes; wired into `_rebuild_config` + `_apply_config_to_ui`). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (17); pick-place/cell-removal/quick-print/print-setup/print-manager/stress/ZP suites green (199). |
| `MEBP_v75x_CELL_TARGETING_REMOVAL.md` | v7.5.x new **Cell Targeting & Removal** workflow (enables the stubbed `cell_targeting` tile) — trypsinize-in-place-then-extract, built on the Spheroid Pick & Place scaffold (reuses LiveTargetPicker pick=removal/place=placement + WorkspaceTargetView + XZSideView + StandardJogContextPanel + the `PickPlaceExecutor` bridge + `run_prep` + safe-Z primitives + `_reagent_prep` service-well helpers; spheroid page untouched). Operator sequence: (1) standard needle prep (waste→oil→wash→buffer, once); (2) load a **cell-release reagent** (e.g. trypsin) assigned to a reagent well; (3) travel to the removal (x,y) + lower to a removal Z (default 0.1 mm off the plate bottom); (4) **slowly push** a small reagent column = `needle.cross_section_area_mm² × push_depth` (default 0.1 mm); (5) wait a user-defined dwell; (6) **quickly pull** `×multiplier` (default 2×) the pushed volume; (7) travel to the user-defined placement + dispense; (8) **clean+reset** (waste→wash→buffer, once after the loop). Steps 1/8 bracket the loop; 2–7 run per picked pair. **Backend (`PickAndPlaceManager`):** new `OperationType.CELL_TARGET_REMOVAL` + `CellRemovalConfig` (`compute_release_volume_uL`/`compute_extract_volume_uL`, slow `push_speed_uL_s`/fast `pull_speed_uL_s`, removal/place Z offsets) + executor `reagent_well_pos`/`reagent_dip_z_mm` (+`__reagent__` resolution) + `do_post_clean`/`post_expel_needles`; `_execute_cell_removal` (load→travel→slow push→dwell→fast pull→travel→gentle dispense, **pump volume-balanced** −push+push−pull+pull=0); `run_post_clean()` run once after the loop in `execute_queue`. Removal/place heights reuse the executor's `pick_z_mm`/`place_z_mm` (zero-ref mm via `print_height_to_zref`, polarity-safe on ZDIR=-1). **GUI (`cell_targeting_workflow.py`, NEW):** cell-removal config row (removal/place Z, push depth, pull ×, dwell, slow push / fast pull speeds, bore), reagent picker (mirrors Quick Print's printable-ink combo), prep + "clean after" rows, Start gate (balanced pairs + Safe Z + ZP connected + calibrated plate bottom + reagent assigned/calibrated + needle inner Ø + service wells when prep/clean on). Every cross-position move routes through `safe_travel_to`; empty-`well_name` targets → full safe-Z travel; mid-run ZP drop → `AbortException`; `finally` ends at safe Z. Wired via `workflow_picker.py` (tile enabled) + `workflows_mode.py`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_cell_targeting_removal.py` (21); spheroid/quick-print/picker-scaling suites green (79). |
| `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_PICK_AND_PLACE_PREP.md` | v7.5.x **Quick Print runs the full bioprint sequence (needle prep → pick up the print's ink → print)** instead of assuming the needle is pre-loaded — mirrors the Spheroid Pick & Place setup. Operator-confirmed scope: **one ink per run** (keep single-object/single-well; the "ink list" = the one ink the selected pump dispenses); **pickup volume computed from the print** (`flow×(path_len/print_speed) + flow×_PREFLOW_S`, ×safety, floored to the prime); **ink override = choose the pump's ink from the library** (combo of printable inks — `ink_type` ∉ {wash,waste,buffer,oil} — that have a reagent location, defaulting to the pump's assigned ink). **Reuse:** `PickPlaceExecutor.run_prep()` (waste→oil→wash→buffer) + safe-Z primitives (`safe_travel_to`/`ensure_retracted_to`/`move_pump_uL`/`print_height_to_zref`/`print_z_dir`) + `build_well_plate_job(path_segments=, return_home=False)` + discrete `PrintManager` (existing print path unchanged). **New** `gui/pages/workflows/_reagent_prep.py` (shared `SERVICE_ROLES`/`service_well_names`/`resolve_service_positions`/`needle_volume_uL` free functions — spheroid page left untouched) + `PickPlaceExecutor.aspirate_ink(well_pos, volume_uL, *, bore, z_mm, rate_uL_s)` (safe-Z travel to `__ink__` + aspirate, abort-aware, ZP-down-guarded). **`quick_print_workflow.py`:** ink-override combo + readout, prep row (prep checkbox **default ON** + Service Z + Wash cycles + Ink dip Z + Pickup safety ×), `_refresh_setup_status` gate, `_compute_pickup_volume_uL`; `_on_print` restructured → gate (XY+ZP connected, ink→calibrated source well, Safe Z + plate bottom, prep service wells) → up-front "confirm setup" dialog → preflight worker (try/finally `_retract_to_safe_z`: `run_prep` → `aspirate_ink` → preposition) → **HANDS-FREE** `_on_prepositioned` (the mid-run position-confirm dialog was REMOVED; the up-front confirm is the sole gate; still refuses on preamble abort/error, mid-run ZP drop, or unconfirmed positioning → auto-abort at safe Z) → `PrintManager`. Turning prep OFF + ink "(none)" restores the legacy plain-print path (no preamble/dialog). Abort routes to executor (preflight) or PrintManager (print); ink/service/cal gates live in `_on_print` (status text), not `_update_button_state` (preserves button-gating tests). **Post-print cleanup (operator follow-on):** after a print COMPLETES, a "Clean needle after print (waste → wash → reset oil)" row (checkbox **default ON** + Waste ×needle (6) + Oil refill ×needle fallback) runs `PickPlaceExecutor.run_print_cleanup()` on a worker thread — expel 6× needle to waste, wash, then refill OIL to reset the syringe's oil level by returning the plunger to its **pre-run position** (`move_pump_uL(bore, baseline − current)`, read live at the oil well; sign-safe via the shared `get_pump_position_uL`/`move_pump_uL` µL↔mm frame; ±20×-needle clamp + fixed-needle fallback). `_on_print` captures the pre-run pump position + gates on waste/wash/oil wells; `_on_state` triggers it on `PrintState.COMPLETED` only (abort/error skip it); reuses Service Z + Wash cycles. **Also (operator follow-on): a `Print speed: N% of max` spin (1–100%, default 25%)** — a single master lever that scales BOTH the XY traverse (`print_speed_mm_s = pct × XY max`) and the pump flow (`flow = pct × Flow@100%`; the Flow knob is relabelled **Flow @100%**) for every print-path segment, so the deposited bead width (volume-per-mm) is invariant to the % (only overall speed changes — `PrintManager._execute_print_path` paces by `max(xy_move_time, pump_move_s)`, both now scale together). 100% anchor = measured XY top speed (`PrintTimingCalibrationStore.get_store().get_xy_max_speed_um_s()`) else `safety_limits.max_xy_speed` (µm/s→mm/s); `_xy_max_mm_s`/`_resolved_print_kinematics` feed both `_build_settings` and `_compute_pickup_volume_uL`; resolved `mm/s · µL/s` shown in the setup status. Scoped to Quick Print. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_quick_print_pick_and_place.py`. |
| `MEBP_v75x_NEEDLE_USE_LAST_KNOWN_LOCATION.md` | v7.5.x **"✓ Use last known location" button on Calibration → Needle Location** so an operator sure nothing changed can skip the camera-based Center & Save re-centering. Scope = **needle reference only** (operator choice): restores the **needle zero** (`zero_position` section), **needle-cam Z** (`device_profile.needle_cam_z` → `controller.set_needle_cam_z_user`), and **saved needle XY** (`device_profile.needle_loc_xy_um`) — all read straight from `settings` (where the needle calibration writes them, so it's immune to live page state) and applied **software-only, NO motion**. Verify-in-view via the existing "⤵ Go to needle location" (separate motion action).
